\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\exp\!\Big\{\alpha\,\frac{\bar{\mathbbm{1}}_{\mathsf{E}}^{\,2}}{\sigma_{\mathsf{E}}^{2}}\Big\}\,\exp\!\Big\{\frac{\alpha\,\operatorname{Cov}(g,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{E}})^{2}}{4\,\|f\|_{\infty}^{2}\,\sigma_{\mathsf{E}}^{2}}\Big\}.

(130)

By the definition of the
\psi_{2}
-norm of
\bar{\mathbbm{1}}_{\mathsf{E}}
,
\mathbb{E}\exp\{\bar{\mathbbm{1}}_{\mathsf{E}}^{\,2}/\sigma_{\mathsf{E}}^{2}\}\leq 2
.
For
0<\alpha\leq 1
, Lyapunov’s inequality gives

\mathbb{E}\exp\!\Big\{\alpha\,\frac{\bar{\mathbbm{1}}_{\mathsf{E}}^{\,2}}{\sigma_{\mathsf{E}}^{2}}\Big\}\leq\big(\mathbb{E}\exp\{\bar{\mathbbm{1}}_{\mathsf{E}}^{\,2}/\sigma_{\mathsf{E}}^{2}\}\big)^{\alpha}\leq 2^{\alpha}.

(131)

Using (
128
) in the second factor of (
130
),

\exp\!\Big\{\frac{\alpha\,\operatorname{Cov}(g,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{E}})^{2}}{4\,\|f\|_{\infty}^{2}\,\sigma_{\mathsf{E}}^{2}}\Big\}\leq\exp\!\Big\{\alpha\,C_{1}\,\frac{\mathtt{L}_{f}^{2}\,p(1-p)}{\|f\|_{\infty}^{2}\,\sigma_{\mathsf{E}}^{2}}\Big\}

(132)

for some absolute
C_{1}>0
.

Combining (
130
)–(
132
) gives

\mathbb{E}\exp\!\Big\{\frac{W^{2}}{S^{2}}\Big\}\leq 2^{\alpha}\exp\!\Big\{\alpha\,A\Big\},\qquad A:=C_{1}\,\frac{\mathtt{L}_{f}^{2}\,p(1-p)}{\|f\|_{\infty}^{2}\,\sigma_{\mathsf{E}}^{2}}.

(133)

Choose

\alpha^{\star}\;:=\;\Big(1+\frac{A}{\ln 2}\Big)^{-1}\in(0,1],

(134)

so that
2^{\alpha^{\star}}\exp\{\alpha^{\star}A\}\leq 2
. With
S^{2}
as in (
129
) at
\alpha=\alpha^{\star}
we obtain

\mathbb{E}\exp\!\Big\{\frac{W^{2}}{S^{2}}\Big\}\leq 2,\qquad\text{hence}\qquad\|W\|_{\psi_{2}}^{2}\;\leq\;S^{2}\;\leq\;C\Big(\|f\|_{\infty}^{2}\,\sigma_{\mathsf{E}}^{2}+\mathtt{L}_{f}^{2}\,p(1-p)\Big),

(135)

for a universal constant
C
(use
1/\alpha^{\star}=1+A/\ln 2
and the definition of
A
).
By the standard sub-Gaussian MGF bound, there exists a universal
c>0
with

\mathbb{E}\!\left[\exp\{3sW\}\right]^{\!1/3}\leq\exp\!\{c\,s^{2}\,\|W\|_{\psi_{2}}^{2}\}\leq\exp\!\{c\,s^{2}(\|f\|_{\infty}^{2}\,\sigma_{\mathsf{E}}^{2}+\mathtt{L}_{f}^{2}\,p(1-p))\}.

(136)

Conclusion.

Combining (
123
), (
124
), (
125
), and (
136
),

\mathbb{E}\!\left[\exp\!\big\{s\big(f(X)\,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{E}}(X)-\mathbb{E}[f(X)\,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{E}}(X)]\big)\big\}\right]\leq\exp\!\left\{c\,s^{2}\Big(p^{2}\,\mathtt{L}_{f}^{2}+\|f\|_{\infty}^{2}\,\sigma_{\mathsf{E}}^{2}\Big)\right\},

(137)

for a universal constant
c>0
. This is the desired sub-Gaussian MGF bound and proves the claimed variance proxy.
∎

B.2
Concentration bounds for random quadratic forms

We now study the concentration of random quadratic forms of the type
X_{1}^{\top}M(X^{-})X_{1}
, where

M:\mathbb{R}^{d\times n}\to\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
. Such quantities naturally appear in the proof of

Proposition

1
. We prove the following lemma.

Lemma 5
.

Let
X\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times n}
satisfy
6
. Then there exists a universal constant
c>0
such that for any
M:\mathbb{R}^{d\times n}\to\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
,

\operatorname{Var}\!\left(X_{1}^{\top}M(X^{-})X_{1}\right)\leq\frac{2}{c}\,\mathbb{E}\!\left[\|M(X^{-})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\frac{2}{c}\|M(X^{-})\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}\right]+\operatorname{Var}\!\left(\operatorname{tr}\!\big(\Sigma_{X}M(X^{-})\big)\right)\;.

(138)

Proof.

By the law of total variance applied to
(X_{1},X^{-})
,

\displaystyle\operatorname{Var}\!\left(X_{1}^{\top}M(X^{-})X_{1}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\!\left[\operatorname{Var}\!\left(X_{1}^{\top}M(X^{-})X_{1}\,\middle|\,X^{-}\right)\right]+\operatorname{Var}\!\left(\mathbb{E}\!\left[X_{1}^{\top}M(X^{-})X_{1}\,\middle|\,X^{-}\right]\right)

(139)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\!\left[\operatorname{Var}\!\left(X_{1}^{\top}M(X^{-})X_{1}\,\middle|\,X^{-}\right)\right]+\operatorname{Var}\!\left(\operatorname{tr}\!\big(\Sigma_{X}M(X^{-})\big)\right)\;,

(140)

where we used
\mathbb{E}[X_{1}^{\top}M(X^{-})X_{1}\mid X^{-}]=\operatorname{tr}(\Sigma_{X}M(X^{-}))
.

It remains to control the first term. Conditionally on
X^{-}
, the Hanson–Wright inequality (see
(
Louart & Couillet 2018
, Remark 2.31)
) yields, almost surely,

\mathbb{P}\!\left(\left|X_{1}^{\top}M(X^{-})X_{1}-\operatorname{tr}\!\big(M(X^{-})\Sigma_{X}\big)\right|\geq t\,\middle|\,X^{-}\right)\leq 2\exp\!\left(-c\min\!\left(\frac{t^{2}}{\|M(X^{-})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}},\,\frac{t}{\|M(X^{-})\|_{\mathrm{op}}}\right)\right)\!.

(141)

Writing the conditional variance in integral form,

\displaystyle\operatorname{Var}\!\left(X_{1}^{\top}M(X^{-})X_{1}\,\middle|\,X^{-}\right)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbb{P}\!\left(\left|X_{1}^{\top}M(X^{-})X_{1}-\operatorname{tr}\!\big(\Sigma_{X}M(X^{-})\big)\right|\geq\sqrt{t}\,\middle|\,X^{-}\right)\,\mathrm{d}t

(142)

\displaystyle\leq 2\!\int_{0}^{\infty}\exp\!\left(-c\,\frac{t}{\|M(X^{-})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}}\right)\mathrm{d}t+2\!\int_{0}^{\infty}\exp\!\left(-c\,\frac{\sqrt{t}}{\|M(X^{-})\|_{\mathrm{op}}}\right)\mathrm{d}t

(143)

\displaystyle=\frac{2\|M(X^{-})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}}{c}+\frac{4\|M(X^{-})\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{c^{2}}\;,

(144)

where we used the change of variables
u=c\sqrt{t}/\|M(X^{-})\|_{\mathrm{op}}
in the second integral. Taking expectations in
X^{-}
gives

\mathbb{E}\!\left[\operatorname{Var}\!\left(X_{1}^{\top}M(X^{-})X_{1}\,\middle|\,X^{-}\right)\right]\leq\frac{2}{c}\,\mathbb{E}\!\left[\|M(X^{-})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\frac{2}{c}\|M(X^{-})\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}\right]\!,

(145)

which, combined with the total-variance decomposition above, completes the proof.
∎

In the special case where the map
M:\mathbb{R}^{d\times n}\to\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
is Lipschitz on
\mathsf{A}_{\eta}
(with
\mathsf{A}_{\eta}
defined in
2
),
Lemma

5
yields:

Proposition 7
.

Let
X\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times n}
satisfy
6
and
2
. For any functions
M_{1}:\mathsf{A}_{\eta}\to\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
and
M_{2}:\mathsf{A}_{\eta}\to\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
that are respectively
\mathtt{L}_{1}
- and
\mathtt{L}_{2}
-Lipschitz and bounded, and any
\mathbf{B}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
with
\|\mathbf{B}\|_{\mathrm{F}}=1
, we have

\operatorname{Var}\!\left(X_{1}^{\top}M_{1}(X^{-})X_{1}\,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right)\lesssim d\,\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}\left\{\mathtt{L}_{1}^{2}+\|M_{1}\|_{\infty}^{2}\,(1+c_{X}^{-1})\right\}\;,

(146)

\operatorname{Var}\!\left(X_{1}^{\top}M_{1}(X^{-})\mathbf{B}\,X_{1}\,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right)\lesssim\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}\left\{\mathtt{L}_{1}^{2}+\|M_{1}\|_{\infty}^{2}\,(1+c_{X}^{-1})\right\}\;,

(147)

\operatorname{Var}\!\left(X_{1}^{\top}M_{1}(X^{-})\mathbf{B}\,M_{2}(X^{-})X_{1}\,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right)\lesssim\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}\!\left\{\big(\|M_{1}\|_{\infty}\mathtt{L}_{2}+\|M_{2}\|_{\infty}\mathtt{L}_{1}\big)^{2}+\|M_{1}\|_{\infty}^{2}\|M_{2}\|_{\infty}^{2}\,(1+c_{X}^{-1})\right\}\;,

(148)

where
\|M_{i}\|_{\infty}=\big\|\|M_{i}(\cdot)\|_{\mathrm{op}}\big\|_{\infty}
.

Proof.

We treat the three cases in the same way. By
Lemma

5
and since
\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)
is
\sigma(X^{-})
-measurable,

\displaystyle\operatorname{Var}\!\left(X_{1}^{\top}M_{1}(X^{-})X_{1}\,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right)

(149)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{2}{c}\,\mathbb{E}\!\left[\|M_{1}(X^{-})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)+\frac{2}{c}\|M_{1}(X^{-})\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}\,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]+\operatorname{Var}\!\left(\operatorname{tr}\!\big(\Sigma_{X}M_{1}(X^{-})\,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\big)\right)

(150)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{2}{c}\!\left(d\|M_{1}\|_{\infty}^{2}+\frac{2}{c}\|M_{1}\|_{\infty}^{2}\right)+\operatorname{Var}\!\left(\operatorname{tr}\!\big(\Sigma_{X}M_{1}(X^{-})\,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\big)\right)\lesssim d\,\|M_{1}\|_{\infty}^{2}+\operatorname{Var}\!\left(\operatorname{tr}\!\big(\Sigma_{X}M_{1}(X^{-})\,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\big)\right)\!.

(151)

Similarly,

\displaystyle\operatorname{Var}\!\left(X_{1}^{\top}M_{1}(X^{-})\mathbf{B}\,X_{1}\,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right)

(152)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{2}{c}\,\mathbb{E}\!\left[\|M_{1}(X^{-})\mathbf{B}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)+\frac{2}{c}\|M_{1}(X^{-})\mathbf{B}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}\,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]+\operatorname{Var}\!\left(\operatorname{tr}\!\big(\Sigma_{X}M_{1}(X^{-})\mathbf{B}\,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\big)\right)

(153)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{2}{c}\!\left(\|M_{1}\|_{\infty}^{2}+\frac{2}{c}\|M_{1}\|_{\infty}^{2}\right)+\operatorname{Var}\!\left(\operatorname{tr}\!\big(\Sigma_{X}M_{1}(X^{-})\mathbf{B}\,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\big)\right)\lesssim\|M_{1}\|_{\infty}^{2}+\operatorname{Var}\!\left(\operatorname{tr}\!\big(\Sigma_{X}M_{1}(X^{-})\mathbf{B}\,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\big)\right)\!.

(154)

Finally,

\displaystyle\operatorname{Var}\!\left(X_{1}^{\top}M_{1}(X^{-})\mathbf{B}\,M_{2}(X^{-})X_{1}\,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right)

(155)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{2}{c}\,\mathbb{E}\!\left[\|M_{1}(X^{-})\mathbf{B}\,M_{2}(X^{-})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)+\frac{2}{c}\|M_{1}(X^{-})\mathbf{B}\,M_{2}(X^{-})\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}\,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]

(156)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\operatorname{Var}\!\left(\operatorname{tr}\!\big(\Sigma_{X}M_{1}(X^{-})\mathbf{B}\,M_{2}(X^{-})\,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\big)\right)

(157)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{2}{c}\!\left(\|M_{1}\|_{\infty}^{2}\|M_{2}\|_{\infty}^{2}+\frac{2}{c}\|M_{1}\|_{\infty}^{2}\|M_{2}\|_{\infty}^{2}\right)+\operatorname{Var}\!\left(\operatorname{tr}\!\big(\Sigma_{X}M_{1}(X^{-})\mathbf{B}\,M_{2}(X^{-})\,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\big)\right)

(158)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\|M_{1}\|_{\infty}^{2}\|M_{2}\|_{\infty}^{2}+\operatorname{Var}\!\left(\operatorname{tr}\!\big(\Sigma_{X}M_{1}(X^{-})\mathbf{B}\,M_{2}(X^{-})\,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\big)\right)\!.

(159)

It remains to bound the trace-variance terms. By Cauchy–Schwarz, for all
\mathbf{X},\mathbf{Y}\in\mathsf{A}_{\eta}
,

\big|\operatorname{tr}\!\big(\Sigma_{X}M_{1}(\mathbf{X}^{-})\big)-\operatorname{tr}\!\big(\Sigma_{X}M_{1}(\mathbf{Y}^{-})\big)\big|\leq\sqrt{d}\,\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\,\mathtt{L}_{1}\,\|\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{Y}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\;,

(160)

\big|\operatorname{tr}\!\big(\Sigma_{X}M_{1}(\mathbf{X}^{-})\mathbf{B}\big)-\operatorname{tr}\!\big(\Sigma_{X}M_{1}(\mathbf{Y}^{-})\mathbf{B}\big)\big|\leq\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\,\mathtt{L}_{1}\,\|\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{Y}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\;,

(161)

and

\displaystyle\left|\operatorname{tr}\!\big(\Sigma_{X}M_{1}(\mathbf{X}^{-})\mathbf{B}\,M_{2}(\mathbf{X}^{-})\big)-\operatorname{tr}\!\big(\Sigma_{X}M_{1}(\mathbf{Y}^{-})\mathbf{B}\,M_{2}(\mathbf{Y}^{-})\big)\right|

(162)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\big(\|M_{1}\|_{\infty}\mathtt{L}_{2}+\|M_{2}\|_{\infty}\mathtt{L}_{1}\big)\,\|\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{Y}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\;.

(163)

Therefore, by
Proposition

6
and standard sub-Gaussian variance bounds,

\operatorname{Var}\!\left(\operatorname{tr}\!\big(\Sigma_{X}M_{1}(X^{-})\big)\right)\lesssim d\,\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}\,\mathtt{L}_{1}^{2}+d\,\|M_{1}\|_{\infty}^{2}+d^{2}\,\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}\,\|M_{1}\|_{\infty}^{2}\,\sigma_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}^{2}\;,

(164)

\operatorname{Var}\!\left(\operatorname{tr}\!\big(\Sigma_{X}M_{1}(X^{-})\mathbf{B}\big)\right)\lesssim\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}\,\mathtt{L}_{1}^{2}+\|M_{1}\|_{\infty}^{2}+d\,\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}\,\|M_{1}\|_{\infty}^{2}\,\sigma_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}^{2}\;,

(165)

and

\displaystyle\operatorname{Var}\!\left(\operatorname{tr}\!\big(\Sigma_{X}M_{1}(X^{-})\mathbf{B}\,M_{2}(X^{-})\big)\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}\big(\|M_{1}\|_{\infty}\mathtt{L}_{2}+\|M_{2}\|_{\infty}\mathtt{L}_{1}\big)^{2}+\|M_{1}\|_{\infty}^{2}\|M_{2}\|_{\infty}^{2}

(166)

\displaystyle\quad+d\,\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}\,\|M_{1}\|_{\infty}^{2}\,\sigma_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}^{2}\;.

(167)

Finally, by
2
,

\sigma_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}^{2}=\frac{1-2\mathbb{P}(X\in\mathsf{A}_{\eta})}{2\log\!\left(\frac{1-\mathbb{P}(X\in\mathsf{A}_{\eta})}{\mathbb{P}(X\in\mathsf{A}_{\eta})}\right)}\lesssim\frac{1}{n\,c_{X}}\,,

(168)

since
1-\mathbb{P}(\mathsf{A}_{\eta})\lesssim\exp(-c_{X}n)
. We also note that
2
forces
d<n
(otherwise
C_{X}
would be rank-deficient a.s., yielding
\mathbb{P}(X\in\mathsf{A}_{\eta})=0
for all
\eta>0
). Plugging the bound on
\sigma_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}^{2}
above into the previous displays gives the stated upper bounds.
∎

B.3
A deterministic equivalent for
\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)
, with arbitrarly small regularization parameter

We first show that the resolvent map is locally Lipschitz, and in particular Lipschitz on
\mathsf{A}_{\eta}
.

Lemma 6
.

Let
\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times n}
and let
\mathbf{D}\succeq 0
. Assume that for
i\in\{1,2\}
,

\uplambda_{d}(C_{\mathbf{X}_{i}}+\mathbf{D})\geq\epsilon>0
. Then

\left\|(C_{\mathbf{X}_{1}}+\mathbf{D})^{-1}-(C_{\mathbf{X}_{2}}+\mathbf{D})^{-1}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\;\leq\;\frac{2}{\sqrt{n\,\epsilon^{3}}}\,\|\mathbf{X}_{1}-\mathbf{X}_{2}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\;.

(169)

Proof.

Using
A^{-1}-B^{-1}=A^{-1}(B-A)B^{-1}
and
\|AB\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\|A\|_{\mathrm{op}}\|B\|_{\mathrm{F}}
,

\displaystyle\left\|(C_{\mathbf{X}_{1}}+\mathbf{D})^{-1}-(C_{\mathbf{X}_{2}}+\mathbf{D})^{-1}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle=\left\|(C_{\mathbf{X}_{1}}+\mathbf{D})^{-1}\big(C_{\mathbf{X}_{2}}-C_{\mathbf{X}_{1}}\big)(C_{\mathbf{X}_{2}}+\mathbf{D})^{-1}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(170)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\left\|(C_{\mathbf{X}_{1}}+\mathbf{D})^{-1}\!\left(\mathbf{X}_{1}(\mathbf{X}_{1}-\mathbf{X}_{2})^{\top}+(\mathbf{X}_{1}-\mathbf{X}_{2})\mathbf{X}_{2}^{\top}\right)\!(C_{\mathbf{X}_{2}}+\mathbf{D})^{-1}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

(171)

Using
\|UV^{\top}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\|U\|_{\mathrm{op}}\|V\|_{\mathrm{F}}
and the triangle inequality,

\displaystyle\cdots\;\leq\;\frac{1}{n}\Big(\|(C_{\mathbf{X}_{1}}+\mathbf{D})^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{1}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\,\|(C_{\mathbf{X}_{2}}+\mathbf{D})^{-1}\|_{\mathrm{op}}+\|(C_{\mathbf{X}_{2}}+\mathbf{D})^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{2}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\,\|(C_{\mathbf{X}_{1}}+\mathbf{D})^{-1}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\Big)\,\|\mathbf{X}_{1}-\mathbf{X}_{2}\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

(172)

Since
\uplambda_{d}(C_{\mathbf{X}_{i}}+\mathbf{D})\geq\epsilon
, we have
\|(C_{\mathbf{X}_{i}}+\mathbf{D})^{-1}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\leq\epsilon^{-1}
.
Moreover,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\,\|(C_{\mathbf{X}_{i}}+\mathbf{D})^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{i}\|_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\uplambda_{d}\!\left((C_{\mathbf{X}_{i}}+\mathbf{D})^{-1}\frac{\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}}{n}(C_{\mathbf{X}_{i}}+\mathbf{D})^{-1}\right)}

(173)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\uplambda_{d}\!\left((C_{\mathbf{X}_{i}}+\mathbf{D})^{-1}C_{\mathbf{X}_{i}}(C_{\mathbf{X}_{i}}+\mathbf{D})^{-1}\right)}\;\leq\;\sqrt{\uplambda_{d}\!\left((C_{\mathbf{X}_{i}}+\mathbf{D})^{-1}\right)}\;\leq\;\epsilon^{-1/2}\;,

(174)

where we used
C_{\mathbf{X}_{i}}\preceq C_{\mathbf{X}_{i}}+\mathbf{D}
.
Plugging these bounds into the previous display yields

\left\|(C_{\mathbf{X}_{1}}+\mathbf{D})^{-1}-(C_{\mathbf{X}_{2}}+\mathbf{D})^{-1}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\;\leq\;\frac{2}{\sqrt{n}}\,\epsilon^{-1/2}\,\epsilon^{-1}\,\|\mathbf{X}_{1}-\mathbf{X}_{2}\|_{\mathrm{F}}=\frac{2}{\sqrt{n\,\epsilon^{3}}}\,\|\mathbf{X}_{1}-\mathbf{X}_{2}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\!,

(175)

as claimed.
∎

In particular.
On
\mathsf{A}_{\eta}=\{\mathbf{X}:\,\uplambda_{d}(C_{\mathbf{X}})\geq\eta\}
(take
\mathbf{D}=0
), the map
\mathbf{X}\mapsto(C_{\mathbf{X}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d})^{-1}
is Lipschitz with constant
2/\sqrt{n(\eta+\lambda)^{3}}
, for all
\lambda\geq 0
.

Define, for any
\mathfrak{b}\in[1,\infty)
and any matrix
\mathbf{D}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
,

\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{b}}(\mathbf{D})\;:=\;\Big(\tfrac{\Sigma_{X}}{\mathfrak{b}}+\mathbf{D}\Big)^{-1}\;.

(176)

We provide two choices of the parameter
\mathfrak{b}
for which
\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{b}}(\mathbf{D})
is a deterministic equivalent of
\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})
. Precisely:

Proposition 8
.

Assume
X
satisfies
6
and
2
for some
\eta>0
.
Let
\mathbf{B}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
and let
\mathbf{D}\succeq 0
be positive semidefinite. Define

\mathfrak{a}^{*}\;=\;1+\frac{1}{n}\,\operatorname{tr}\!\left(\Sigma_{X}\,\mathbb{E}\!\left[\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right),

(177)

and
\mathfrak{b}^{*}
be the unique fixed point of

f_{\mathbf{D}}:\ \mathfrak{b}\mapsto 1+\frac{1}{n}\operatorname{tr}\!\left(\Sigma_{X}\,\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{b}}(\mathbf{D})\right).

(178)

Then, for an absolute constant
k>0
and all
t>0
,

\mathbb{P}\!\left(\left|\frac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\!\left(\mathbf{B}\!\left\{\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)-\mathbb{E}\!\left[\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\}\right)\right|\geq t\right)\;\lesssim\;\exp\!\left(-\;k\,\frac{c_{X}\,(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3}\,n\,t^{2}}{\|\mathbf{B}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}\,\big(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})+c_{X}/d\big)}\right)\!.

(179)

Furthermore, define the polynomial
Q:\mathbb{R}^{5}\to\mathbb{R}
by

Q(X,Y,Z,U,V)\;=\;(1+UX+VX)\,(X^{3}Z+X^{2}+YX^{2}Z+YX)\;+\;YX^{2}Z\;+\;YX^{4}Y^{2}\;,

(180)

and set
q=Q\big(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}),\,\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X}),\,\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{-1},\,c_{X}^{-1},\,n^{-1}\big)
. Then

\left\|\mathbb{E}\!\left[\big\{\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})-\bar{\operatorname{R}}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\big\}\,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\;\lesssim\;\frac{q\,\sqrt{d}\,\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\,\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})\,(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{6}}\;,

(181)

and

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\!\left[\big\{\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})-\bar{\operatorname{R}}^{\mathfrak{b}^{*}}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\big\}\,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\;\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\left(1+\frac{d\,\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{n\,\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})}\right)\Bigg(\frac{q\,\sqrt{d}\,\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\,\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})\,(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{6}}

(182)

\displaystyle\hskip 62.00006pt+\left(\frac{d\,\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{n}+\frac{d^{2}\,\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))\,n^{2}}\right)\,\mathrm{e}^{-c_{X}n}\Bigg)\;.

(183)

Remark.
This result generalizes those of
Chouard 2022
. Firstly, under
2
one may take an arbitrarily small regularization
\mathbf{D}\succeq 0
and still retain favorable concentration properties for the resolvent (even
\mathbf{D}=0
), secondly we provide fully explicit bounds which allow to understand deeper the dependancies to all the parameters.
Our proof follows
Chouard 2022
closely.

Proof.

The proof procees in two parts, first we derive the claimed concentration bound for terms of the form
d^{-1}\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbf{B}\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\right)
, which follows from concentration of Lipschitz transformations of
X
(
6
), as well as
Proposition

6
. Then we will derive the claimed bias bound, using the Shermann-Morison formula.

Concentration of
d^{-1}\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbf{B}\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)

We mainly rely on
Proposition

6
, first note that the map

h_{\mathbf{B},\mathbf{D}}:\begin{cases}\mathsf{A}_{\eta}&\to\mathbb{R}\\
\mathbf{X}&\mapsto\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbf{B}\operatorname{R}_{\mathbf{X}}(\mathbf{D})\right)\end{cases}\;,

(184)

is
2\|\mathbf{B}\|_{\mathrm{op}}(\eta+\lambda_{1}(\mathbf{D}))^{-3/2}n^{-1/2}d^{-1/2}
-Lipschitz from
Lemma

6
. Moreover
\|h_{\mathbf{B},\mathbf{D}}\|_{\infty}\leq\|\mathbf{B}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}(\eta+\lambda_{1}(\mathbf{D}))^{-1}
, we have from
Proposition

6
that
h_{\mathbf{B},\mathbf{D}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)
is sub Gaussian, with parameter,

\sigma_{h_{\mathbf{B},\mathbf{D}}(X)}^{2}\lesssim\mathbb{P}(X\in\mathsf{A}_{\eta})^{2}\dfrac{\|\mathbf{B}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3}nd}+\dfrac{\|\mathbf{B}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}\sigma(\mathbb{P}(X\in\mathsf{A}_{\eta}))^{2}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{2}}\;,

(185)

and, remarking that by definition of
\sigma
given in
Proposition

6
, since
\eta
satisfies
2
, we have,

\sigma(\mathbb{P}(X\in\mathsf{E}))^{2}\lesssim\dfrac{1}{nc_{X}}\;,

(186)

which implies that

\displaystyle\sigma_{h_{\mathbf{B},\mathbf{D}}}^{2}\lesssim\dfrac{\|\mathbf{B}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{nd(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3}}+\dfrac{\|\mathbf{B}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{c_{X}n(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{2}}=\dfrac{\|\mathbf{B}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{c_{X}n(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3}}\left(\dfrac{c_{X}}{d}+(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))\right)\;,

(187)

hence, using the variance bound for sub Gaussian random variable, we have for a universal constant
k
,

\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbf{B}\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)-\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbf{B}\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right|\geq t\right)\lesssim\exp\left(-k\dfrac{t^{2}c_{X}(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3}nt^{2}}{\|\mathbf{B}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}+c_{X}/d))}\right)\;,

(188)

First equivalent for
\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]

Recall the notation
\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})=R_{X^{-}}(\mathbf{D})
where
X^{-}=[0,X_{1},\cdots,X_{n}]
, we have from the Shermann-Morison formula
Sherman & Morrison 1950
,

\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)=\left\{\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})-\dfrac{1}{n}\dfrac{\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})}{1+n^{-1}X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}}\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\;.

(189)

hence, multiplying both sides by
X_{1}
, we obtain,

\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)=\dfrac{\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}}{1+n^{-1}X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\;.

(190)

Denoting
\mathfrak{a}_{X}=1+n^{-1}X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)
, we simplify the above expression to,

\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)=\dfrac{\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}}{\mathfrak{a}_{X}}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\;.

(191)

We now focus on bounding the bias of
\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)
. First, using the identity
\mathbf{A}^{-1}-\mathbf{B}^{-1}=\mathbf{A}^{-1}(\mathbf{B}-\mathbf{A})\mathbf{B}^{-1}
, we have,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})-\bar{\operatorname{R}}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\left\{\dfrac{\Sigma_{X}}{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}-C_{X}\right\}\bar{\operatorname{R}}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]

(192)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\left\{\dfrac{\Sigma_{X}}{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}-X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\right\}\bar{\operatorname{R}}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]

(193)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\Sigma_{X}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}(\mathbf{D})-\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{X}}\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}(\mathbf{D})\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]

(194)

where, in the last equality, we have used (
191
). Further rearranging the terms, we get,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})-\bar{\operatorname{R}}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]

(195)

\displaystyle\quad=\mathbb{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}\{\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})-\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})\}\Sigma_{X}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)+\left(\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}-\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{X}}\right)\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\;,

(196)

Hence, by applying the triangle inequality to bound the bias, we obtain,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})-\bar{\operatorname{R}}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(197)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\{\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})-\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\dfrac{\Sigma_{X}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}(\mathbf{D})}{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}-\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{X}}\right)\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(198)

Controlling each term individualy, we first use
\|\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\|\mathbf{A}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\|\mathbf{B}\|_{\mathrm{F}}
to get,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\{\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})-\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\dfrac{\Sigma_{X}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}(\mathbf{D})}{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\{\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})-\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\left\|\dfrac{\Sigma_{X}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{a}}(\mathbf{D})}{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}

(199)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\{\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})-\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\;,

(200)

From (
191
),

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\{\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})-\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}=\dfrac{1}{n}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{X}}\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\;,

(201)

In order to easily bound the riht-hand side of the previous inequality, we introduce the Lowner order on symetrix matrices. We say that
\mathbf{A}\preceq\mathbf{B}
if and only if
\mathbf{B}-\mathbf{A}
is a PSD matrix, then we have
\mathfrak{a}_{X}^{-1}\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\preceq\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)
almost surely. It is clear that this ordering is preserved when averaging the matrices, we get

\mathbb{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{X}}\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\preceq\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]

(202)

and, using the fact that the Frobenius norm is non-decreasing w.r.t. the Lowner order on PSD matrices, we deduce,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\{\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})-\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{n}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{X}}\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(203)

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{1}{n}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(204)

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{1}{n}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})\Sigma_{X}\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(205)

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{n(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{2}}\;.

(206)

Plugging the previous computations in (
198
), we get

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})-\bar{\operatorname{R}}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(207)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}-\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{X}}\right)\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{n(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{2}}\;.

(208)

It remains only to bound the second term in the right hand side of (
198
), recalling the dual representation of the Frobenius norm,

\|\mathbf{A}\|_{\mathrm{F}}=\sup_{\|\mathbf{B}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq 1}\operatorname{tr}(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{A})\;,

(209)

we define for any
\mathbf{B}
of unit Frobenius norm, the random variable
\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}=X_{1}^{\top}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbf{B}\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}-\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{X}}\right)\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(210)

\displaystyle\quad=\sup_{\|\mathbf{B}\|_{\mathrm{F}}=1}\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbf{B}^{\top}\left(\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}-\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{X}}\right)\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right)\right]

(211)

\displaystyle\quad=\sup_{\|\mathbf{B}\|_{\mathrm{F}}=1}\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbf{B}\left(\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}-\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{X}}\right)\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right)\right]\right|

(212)

\displaystyle\quad=\sup_{\|\mathbf{B}\|_{\mathrm{F}}=1}\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}-\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{X}}\right)\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}\right]\right|

(213)

To conclude the proof, we use
proposition

7
to bound the variances of
\mathfrak{a}_{X}
as well as
\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}
, to bound the above term uniformly over all the possible choices of
\mathbf{B}
.
Using the triangle inequality, we have,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}-\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{X}}\right)\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\left(\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}-\dfrac{1}{\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{a}_{X}\right]}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}\right]\right|+\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\dfrac{1}{\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{a}_{X}\right]}-\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{X}}\right)\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}\right]\right|

(215)

We further rewrite the first term by remarking that
\mathfrak{a}_{X}\geq 1
almost surely, and similarly
\mathfrak{a}^{*}
, we get:

\displaystyle\left|\left(\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}-\dfrac{1}{\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{a}_{X}\right]}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq|\mathfrak{a}^{*}-\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{a}_{X}\right]|\dfrac{\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}\right]}{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}\;.

(216)

Now, observe that
|\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}\right]|
may be explicitly controlled by,

\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}\right]/\mathfrak{a}^{*}|

\displaystyle=\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{tr}\left(\dfrac{\Sigma_{X}}{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbf{B}\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right)\right]\right|

(217)

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{\sqrt{d}}{\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}\;.

(218)

Where we have used the fact that
\Sigma_{X}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}(\lambda)/\mathfrak{a}^{*}\preceq\operatorname{I}_{d}
. Secondly, the bias of
\mathfrak{a}_{X}
can be bounded using (
189
) as,

\displaystyle|\mathfrak{a}^{*}-\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{a}_{X}\right]|

\displaystyle=\left|\dfrac{1}{n}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})-\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\right]\right)\right|=\dfrac{1}{n^{2}}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\mathbb{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{X}}\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})\right]\right)

(219)

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{1}{n^{2}}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})\right]\right)\leq\dfrac{1}{n^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{tr}\left((\Sigma_{X}\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D}))^{2}\right)\right]

(220)

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{d\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{2}n^{2}}\;.

(221)

Which implies the following bound on the first term in (
215
),

\left|\left(\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}-\dfrac{1}{\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{a}_{X}\right]}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}\right]\right|\lesssim\dfrac{d^{3/2}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3}n^{2}}\;.

(222)

Now dealing with the second term in (
215
), using the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, as well as
\mathbb{E}[\mathfrak{a}_{X}]\geq 1
, we have,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\dfrac{1}{\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{a}_{X}\right]}-\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{X}}\right)\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}\right]\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\mathbb{E}\left[(\mathfrak{a}_{X}-\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{a}_{X}\right])\dfrac{\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}}{\mathfrak{a}_{X}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{a}_{X}\right]}\right]\right|\leq\sqrt{\operatorname{Var}\left(\mathfrak{a}_{X}\right)\operatorname{Var}\left(\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}/\mathfrak{a}_{X}\right)}

(223)

We write
\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}/\mathfrak{a}_{X}=(\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}-\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}\right])/\mathfrak{a}_{X}+\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}\right]/\mathfrak{a}_{X}
, and using
\operatorname{Var}(a+b)\leq 2\operatorname{Var}(a)+2\operatorname{Var}(b)
, we have,

\displaystyle\operatorname{Var}\left(\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}/\mathfrak{a}_{X}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\operatorname{Var}\left((\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}-\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}\right])/\mathfrak{a}_{X}\right)+2\operatorname{Var}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}\right]/\mathfrak{a}_{X}\right)

(224)

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{E}\left[\left((\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}-\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}\right])/\mathfrak{a}_{X}\right)^{2}\right]+2\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}\right]^{2}\operatorname{Var}\left(\mathfrak{a}_{X}^{-1}\right)

(225)

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}-\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}\right]\right)^{2}\right]+2\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}\right]^{2}\operatorname{Var}\left(\mathfrak{a}_{X}^{-1}\right)

(226)

\displaystyle\leq 2\operatorname{Var}(\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X})+2\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}\right]^{2}\operatorname{Var}(\mathfrak{a}_{X}^{-1})\;.

(227)

Furthermore,
\operatorname{Var}(\mathfrak{a}_{X}^{-1})=\operatorname{inf}_{m}\mathbb{E}\left[(\mathfrak{a}_{X}^{-1}-m)^{2}\right]\leq\mathbb{E}\left[(\mathfrak{a}_{X}^{-1}-\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{a}_{X}\right]^{-1})^{2}\right]\leq\operatorname{Var}(\mathfrak{a}_{X})
, which follows from
\mathfrak{a}_{X}\geq 1
again, we get,

\operatorname{Var}\left(\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}/\mathfrak{a}_{X}\right)\leq 2\operatorname{Var}\left(\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}\right)+2\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}\right]^{2}\operatorname{Var}(\mathfrak{a}_{X})\;,

(228)

which results in,

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\dfrac{1}{\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{a}_{X}\right]}-\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{X}}\right)\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}\right]^{2}\leq 2\operatorname{Var}\left(\mathfrak{a}_{X}\right)\operatorname{Var}\left(\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}\right)+2\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}\right]^{2}\operatorname{Var}\left(\mathfrak{a}_{X}\right)^{2}

(229)

We conclude by bounding
\operatorname{Var}(\mathfrak{a}_{X})
and
\operatorname{Var}(\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X})
, using
Proposition

7
and the Lipschitz property of
\mathbf{X}\mapsto\operatorname{R}_{\mathbf{X}}^{-}(\mathbf{D})
on
\mathsf{A}_{\eta}
(which results from
Lemma

6
), we have,

\displaystyle\operatorname{Var}\left(\mathfrak{a}_{X}\right)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{n^{2}}\operatorname{Var}\left(X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right)\lesssim\dfrac{d\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{n^{2}}\left\{\dfrac{1}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3}n}+\dfrac{1+c_{X}^{-1}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{2}}\right\}

(230)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{d\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{n^{2}(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3}}\left\{\dfrac{1}{n}+\left(1+\dfrac{1}{c_{X}}\right)(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))\right\}\;,

(231)

similarly,

\displaystyle\operatorname{Var}\left(\zeta_{\mathbf{D},X}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim X_{1}^{\top}\bar{\operatorname{R}}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\mathbf{B}\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)

(232)

\displaystyle\lesssim\dfrac{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}\|\bar{\operatorname{R}}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3}}\left\{\dfrac{1}{n}+\left(1+\dfrac{1}{c_{X}}\right)(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))\right\}\;,

(233)

it results from the two previous upper bounds, as well as (
218
) and (
229
), that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\dfrac{1}{\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{a}_{X}\right]}-\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{X}}\right)\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}\|\bar{\operatorname{R}}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{n(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3}}\left\{\dfrac{1}{n}+\left(1+\dfrac{1}{c_{X}}\right)(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))\right\}

(234)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{d\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}\right]}{n^{2}(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3}}\left\{\dfrac{1}{n}+\left(1+\dfrac{1}{c_{X}}(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))\right)\right\}

(235)

\displaystyle\lesssim\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}\|\bar{\operatorname{R}}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{n(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3}}\left\{\dfrac{1}{n}+\left(1+\dfrac{1}{c_{X}}\right)(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))\right\}

(236)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{\mathfrak{a}^{*}d^{3/2}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{n^{2}(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{4}}\left\{\dfrac{1}{n}+\left(1+\dfrac{1}{c_{X}}(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))\right)\right\}\;.

(237)

Finally, we bound
\|\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}(\mathbf{D})\|_{\mathrm{op}}
and
\mathfrak{a}^{*}
by writing

\displaystyle\mathfrak{a}^{*}

\displaystyle=1+\dfrac{1}{n}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right)\leq 1+\dfrac{d\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{n(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))}\;,

(238)

and

\displaystyle\|\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}(\mathbf{D})\|_{\mathrm{op}}\leq\left(\dfrac{\lambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})}{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}+\lambda_{1}(\mathbf{D})\right)^{-1}\leq\dfrac{\mathfrak{a}}{\lambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})}\leq\dfrac{1+\dfrac{d}{n}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{-1}}{\lambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})}\;.

(239)

We conclude from (
234
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\dfrac{1}{\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{a}_{X}\right]}-\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{X}}\right)\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{n\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3}}\left\{\dfrac{1}{n}+\left(1+\dfrac{1}{c_{X}}\right)(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))\right\}

(240)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{d^{3/2}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n^{2}\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{4}}\left\{\dfrac{1}{n}+\left(1+\dfrac{1}{c_{X}}\right)(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))\right\}

(241)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{d^{3/2}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{n^{2}(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{4}}\left\{\dfrac{1}{n}+\left(1+\dfrac{1}{c_{X}}(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))\right)\right\}

(242)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{d^{5/2}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n^{6}(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{5}}\left\{\dfrac{1}{n}+\left(1+\dfrac{1}{c_{X}}(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))\right)\right\}\;.

(243)

We slighly simplify the previous upper bound by remarking that
2
implies that
d<n
, hence:

\dfrac{d^{5/2}}{n^{3}}\leq\dfrac{d^{3/2}}{n^{2}}\leq\dfrac{\sqrt{d}}{n}\;,

(244)

Using this in (
240
), we obtain,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\dfrac{1}{\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{a}_{X}\right]}-\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{X}}\right)\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{5}}\left\{\dfrac{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{2}}{n\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}+\left(1+\dfrac{1}{c_{X}}\right)\dfrac{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3}}{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}\right\}

(245)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{5}}\left\{\dfrac{\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}{n}+\left(1+\dfrac{1}{c_{X}}\right)(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{2}\right\}

(246)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{5}}\left\{\dfrac{\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))}{n\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}+\left(1+\dfrac{1}{c_{X}}\right)\dfrac{\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{2}}{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}\right\}

(247)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{5}}\left\{\dfrac{\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}{n}+\left(1+\dfrac{1}{c_{X}}\right)\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))\right\}\;.

(248)

Defining the polynomial
P_{1}
as:

P_{1}(X,Y,Z)=X^{3}Z+X^{2}+YX^{2}Z+YX\;,\quad p_{1}=P_{1}(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}),\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X}),\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{-1})\;,

(249)

we can rewrite the previous upper bound as,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\dfrac{1}{\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{a}_{X}\right]}-\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{X}}\right)\zeta_{\mathbf{B},X}\right]^{2}

(250)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{5}}p_{1}\left(\dfrac{1}{n(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))}+1+\dfrac{1}{c_{X}}\right)\;.

(251)

Plugging (
222
) and (
250
) in (
215
), we get,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}-\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{X}}\right)\operatorname{R}_{X}^{-}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(252)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\dfrac{d^{3/2}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3}n^{2}}+\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{5}}p_{1}\left(\dfrac{1}{n(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))}+1+\dfrac{1}{c_{X}}\right)

(253)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{5}}\left\{p_{1}\left(\dfrac{1}{n(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))}+1+\dfrac{1}{c_{X}}\right)+\dfrac{\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{2}}{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}\right\}

(254)

Where, once again, we have used the fact that
d<n
from
2
.

Finally, from (
207
), we have,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})-\bar{\operatorname{R}}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(255)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{2}n}+\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{5}}\left\{p_{1}\left(\dfrac{1}{n(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))}+1+\dfrac{1}{c_{X}}\right)+\dfrac{\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{2}}{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}\right\}

(256)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{6}}\Biggl\{\dfrac{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))p_{1}}{n}+(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))p_{1}\left(1+\dfrac{1}{c_{X}}\right)

(257)

\displaystyle\hskip 128.0374pt+\dfrac{\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3}}{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}+\dfrac{\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})^{4})}{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}\Biggr\}\;.

(258)

Defining
Q
as,

\displaystyle Q(X,Y,Z,U,V)=XVP(X,Y,Z)+\left(1+U\right)XP(X,Y,Z)+YX^{3}Z+YX^{4}Y^{2}\;,

(259)

We have shown that:

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})-\bar{\operatorname{R}}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{6}}Q(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}),\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X}),\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{-1},c_{X}^{-1},n^{-1})\;,

(260)

This concludes the proof for the first deterministic equivalent.

Second equivalent for
\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{E}}(X)\right]

Now, we show that:

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})-\bar{\operatorname{R}}^{\mathfrak{b}^{*}}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(261)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\left(1+\dfrac{d\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{n\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})}\right)\left(\dfrac{q\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{6}}+\left(\dfrac{d\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{n}+\dfrac{d^{2}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))n^{2}}\right)\mathrm{e}^{-c_{X}n}\right)\;,

(262)

where
\mathfrak{b}^{*}
is defined as the only positive solution to equation
\mathfrak{b}=1+n^{-1}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{b}}(\mathbf{D})\right)
. Recall the definition of
f_{\mathbf{D}}
, for any
\mathfrak{b}\in[1,\infty)
,

f_{\mathbf{D}}(\mathfrak{b})=1+n^{-1}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{b}}(\mathbf{D})\right)\;.

(263)

For notation simplicity, we introduce
q\in\mathbb{R}
defined as:

q=Q(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}),\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X}),\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{-1},c_{X}^{-1},n^{-1})\;,

(264)

where
Q
is defined in (
259
).

First, using (
260
), we have,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})-\bar{\operatorname{R}}^{\mathfrak{b}^{*}}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(265)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})-\bar{\operatorname{R}}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\bar{\operatorname{R}}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}_{X}(\mathbf{D})-\bar{\operatorname{R}}^{\mathfrak{b}^{*}}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(266)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\dfrac{q\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{6}}+\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\bar{\operatorname{R}}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}_{X}(\mathbf{D})-\bar{\operatorname{R}}^{\mathfrak{b}^{*}}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\;,

(267)

Then, using the identity
\mathbf{A}^{-1}-\mathbf{B}^{-1}=\mathbf{A}^{-1}(\mathbf{B}-\mathbf{A})\mathbf{B}^{-1}
we deduce

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\bar{\operatorname{R}}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}_{X}(\mathbf{D})-\bar{\operatorname{R}}^{\mathfrak{b}^{*}}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle=\left|\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a^{*}}}-\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{b^{*}}}\right|\mathbb{P}(X\in\mathsf{A}_{\eta})\left\|\bar{\operatorname{R}}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\Sigma_{X}\bar{\operatorname{R}}^{\mathfrak{b}^{*}}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(268)

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\mathfrak{a}^{*}-\mathfrak{b}^{*}\right|\|\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}(\mathbf{D})\|_{\mathrm{F}}/\mathfrak{a}^{*}

(269)

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\mathfrak{a}^{*}-\mathfrak{b}^{*}\right|\dfrac{\sqrt{d}}{\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})}\;,

(270)

Furthermore, we remark that
\mathfrak{a}^{*}
is almost a fixed point of
f_{\mathbf{D}}
from the first deterministic equivalent, indeed,

\displaystyle|\mathfrak{a}^{*}-f_{\mathbf{D}}(\mathfrak{a}^{*})|

\displaystyle=n^{-1}\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]-\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}(\mathbf{D})\right\}\right)\right|

(271)

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{n}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]-\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}(\mathbf{D})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(272)

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{n}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})-\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\dfrac{d\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\|\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}(\mathbf{D})\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{n}(1-\mathbb{P}(X\in\mathsf{A}_{\eta}))

(273)

Recalling (
239
), and using
2
, we have,

\displaystyle\dfrac{d\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\|\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}(\mathbf{D})\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{n}(1-\mathbb{P}(X\in\mathsf{A}_{\eta}))\leq\left(\dfrac{d\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{n}+\dfrac{d^{2}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))n^{2}}\right)\mathrm{e}^{-c_{X}n}\;,

(275)

Now, using equation (
260
), we have,

\displaystyle\left|\mathfrak{a}^{*}-f_{\mathbf{D}}(\mathfrak{a}^{*})\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\dfrac{q\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{6}}+\left(\dfrac{d\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{n}+\dfrac{d^{2}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))n^{2}}\right)\mathrm{e}^{-c_{X}n}\;.

(276)

Furthermore, we show that
f_{\mathbf{D}}
is a contraction mapping around
\mathfrak{b}^{*}
, indeed we have for any
\mathfrak{b}\in[1,\infty)
,

\displaystyle\left|f_{\mathbf{D}}(\mathfrak{b})-f_{\mathbf{D}}(\mathfrak{b}^{*})\right|

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{n}\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\left\{\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{b}}(\mathbf{D})-\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{b}^{*}}(\mathbf{D})\right\}\right)\right|=\dfrac{1}{n}\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{b}}(\mathbf{D})\Sigma_{X}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{b}^{*}}(\mathbf{D})\right)\right|\left|\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{b}}-\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{b}^{*}}\right|

(277)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{n}\operatorname{tr}\left(\left\{\operatorname{I}_{d}-\mathbf{D}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathbf{b}}(\mathbf{D})\right\}\dfrac{\Sigma_{X}}{\mathfrak{b}^{*}}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{b}^{*}}(\mathbf{D})\right)\left|\mathfrak{b}-\mathfrak{b}^{*}\right|

(278)

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{1}{n\mathfrak{b}^{*}}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{b}^{*}}(\mathbf{D})\right)\left|\mathfrak{b}-\mathfrak{b}^{*}\right|

(279)

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{f_{\mathbf{D}}(\mathfrak{b}^{*})-1}{f_{\mathbf{D}}(\mathfrak{b}^{*})}|\mathfrak{b}-\mathfrak{b}^{*}|=\dfrac{\mathfrak{b}^{*}-1}{\mathfrak{b}^{*}}|\mathfrak{b}-\mathfrak{b}^{*}|\;,

(280)

where in the last line, we have used the fact that
\mathfrak{b}^{*}
is the only fixed point of
f_{\mathbf{D}}
. We conclude that
f_{\mathbf{D}}
is contractive around
\mathfrak{b}^{*}
. We conclude on the distance between
\mathfrak{a}^{*}
and
\mathfrak{b}^{*}
by writing,

|\mathfrak{a}^{*}-\mathfrak{b}^{*}|\leq|\mathfrak{a}^{*}-f_{\mathbf{D}}(\mathfrak{a}^{*})|+|f_{\mathbf{D}}(\mathfrak{a}^{*})-f_{\mathbf{D}}(\mathfrak{b}^{*})|\leq|\mathfrak{a}^{*}-f_{\mathbf{D}}(\mathfrak{a}^{*})|+\dfrac{\mathfrak{b}^{*}-1}{\mathfrak{b}^{*}}|\mathfrak{a}^{*}-\mathfrak{b}^{*}|\;,

(281)

which implies,

|\mathfrak{a}^{*}-\mathfrak{b}^{*}|\leq\mathfrak{b}^{*}|\mathfrak{a}^{*}-f_{\mathbf{D}}(\mathfrak{a}^{*})|\;.

(282)

To conclude the proof, we need to bound
\mathfrak{b}^{*}
. To this end, write

\displaystyle\mathfrak{b}^{*}

\displaystyle=1+\dfrac{1}{n}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{b}^{*}}(\mathbf{D})\right)\leq 1+\dfrac{1}{n}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{1}(\mathbf{D})\right)\leq 1+\dfrac{d\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{n\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})}\;,

(283)

which followed from
\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{b}^{*}}(\mathbf{D})\preceq\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{1}(\mathbf{D})
, we obtain from (
268
),

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\bar{\operatorname{R}}^{\mathfrak{a}^{*}}_{X}(\mathbf{D})-\bar{\operatorname{R}}^{\mathfrak{b}^{*}}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{E}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(284)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\left(1+\dfrac{d\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{n\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})}\right)\left(\dfrac{q\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{6}}+\left(\dfrac{d\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{n}+\dfrac{d^{2}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))n^{2}}\right)\mathrm{e}^{-c_{X}n}\right)

(285)

Finally, from (
265
), we have,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\operatorname{R}_{X}(\mathbf{D})-\bar{\operatorname{R}}^{\mathfrak{b}^{*}}_{X}(\mathbf{D})\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{E}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(286)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\left(1+\dfrac{d\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{n\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})}\right)\left(\dfrac{q\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{6}}+\left(\dfrac{d\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{n}+\dfrac{d^{2}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))n^{2}}\right)\mathrm{e}^{-c_{X}n}\right)\;.

(287)

This concludes the proof.
∎

B.4
Conclusion on the proof of
theorem

1

We leverage
proposition

1
, to prove that
\hat{\mathcal{E}}_{X}(\lambda)
approximates
\mathcal{E}_{X}(\lambda)
. In all this proof, we set
X\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times n}
and
\eta>0
, such that
6
and
2
are satisfied. We first recall,

\mathcal{E}_{X}(\lambda)=\|\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)-\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\;,\quad\text{for }\lambda>0\;,

(288)

and

\hat{\mathcal{E}}_{X}(\lambda):=\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)^{2}\right)-\dfrac{2(1-d/n)}{\lambda d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(0)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)+\dfrac{2}{\lambda\mathfrak{b}(\lambda)d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\right)+\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}^{-2}\right)\;,

(289)

where

\mathfrak{b}(\lambda):=\dfrac{1}{1-d/n+\lambda/n\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\right)}\;.

(290)

We will write
\Delta\mathcal{E}_{X}(\lambda)=\hat{\mathcal{E}}_{X}(\lambda)-\mathcal{E}_{X}(\lambda)
, and we remark that,

\Delta\mathcal{E}_{X}(\lambda)=-\dfrac{2(1-d/n)}{\lambda d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(0)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)+\dfrac{2}{\lambda\mathfrak{b}(\lambda)d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\right)-\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\right)\;.

(291)

We derive the claimed concentration bound by applying
proposition

1
, first noting that
\Delta\mathcal{E}_{X}(\lambda)
is close to the following quantity with high probability,

\displaystyle\overline{\Delta\mathcal{E}_{X}}(\lambda)

\displaystyle=-\dfrac{2(1-d/n)}{\lambda d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(0)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)+\dfrac{2}{\lambda\mathfrak{b}(\lambda)d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)-\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)

(292)

indeed, we have for all
\epsilon>0
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\Delta\mathcal{E}_{X}(\lambda)-\overline{\Delta\mathcal{E}_{X}}(\lambda)\right|\geq\epsilon\right)\leq\mathbb{P}\left(X\notin\mathsf{A}_{\eta}\right)\lesssim\mathrm{e}^{-c_{X}n}\;.

(293)

Furthermore, we show that each term in
\overline{\Delta\mathcal{E}_{X}}(\lambda)
concentrates around its expectation. We have,

\displaystyle\left|\overline{\Delta\mathcal{E}_{X}}(\lambda)-\mathbb{E}\left[\overline{\Delta\mathcal{E}_{X}}(\lambda)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{2(1-d/n)}{\lambda d}\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(0)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)-\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(0)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right|

(294)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{2\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\right)}{\lambda d}\left|\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{b}(\lambda)}-\mathbb{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{b}(\lambda)}\right]\right|

(295)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\left[\dfrac{2}{\lambda\mathfrak{b}(\lambda)d}\right]\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)-\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right|

(296)

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)-\mathbb{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right|

(297)

Remarking that
\eta>0
implies that
d<n
hence,
0\leq 1-d/n\leq 1
, as well as
\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\preceq\lambda^{-1}\operatorname{I}_{d}
and
\mathfrak{b}(\lambda)\geq 1
, we bound the various multiplicative term in the previous inequation as,

\displaystyle\left|\overline{\Delta\mathcal{E}_{X}}(\lambda)-\mathbb{E}\left[\overline{\Delta\mathcal{E}_{X}}(\lambda)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{2}{\lambda d}\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(0)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)-\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(0)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right|

(298)

\displaystyle\quad+2\lambda\dfrac{1}{n}\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)-\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\right)\right]\right)\right|+\dfrac{2}{\lambda d}\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\right)\right]\right|

(299)

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\right)\right]\right|

(300)

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{2}{\lambda d}\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(0)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)-\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(0)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right|

(301)

\displaystyle\quad+2\left\{\lambda+\dfrac{1}{\lambda}\right\}\dfrac{1}{d}\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)-\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right)\right|

(302)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{1}{d}\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)-\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right|

(303)

Hence, from a union bound argument, we have,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\overline{\Delta\mathcal{E}_{X}}(\lambda)-\mathbb{E}\left[\overline{\Delta\mathcal{E}_{X}}(\lambda)\right]\right|\geq t\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\dfrac{1}{d}\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(0)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)-\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{X}(0)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right)\right|\geq\dfrac{\lambda t}{6}\right)

(305)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{P}\left(\dfrac{1}{d}\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)-\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right)\right|\geq\dfrac{t}{6(\lambda+\lambda^{-1})}\right)

(306)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{P}\left(\dfrac{1}{d}\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\{\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)-\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\}\right)\right|\geq\dfrac{t}{3}\right)\;.

(307)

We now control each of these term, by using the concentration statement of
proposition

1
every time. We denote,

\displaystyle\xi_{X}(t)=\dfrac{c_{X}\eta^{3}nt^{2}}{\max\{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}},1\}^{2}(\eta+c_{X}/d)}\;.

(308)

Then,
Proposition

1
and (
305
) implies that,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\overline{\Delta\mathcal{E}_{X}}(\lambda)-\mathbb{E}\left[\overline{\Delta\mathcal{E}_{X}}(\lambda)\right]\right|\geq t\right)\lesssim\mathrm{e}^{-k\xi(\lambda t/6)}+\mathrm{e}^{-k\xi(t/(6(\lambda+\lambda^{-1})))}+\mathrm{e}^{-k\xi(t/3)}\;,

(309)

for a universal constant
k>0
. Now remarking that
\xi(\lambda t/6)\geq\xi(t/(6(\lambda+\lambda^{-1})))
, we have,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\overline{\Delta\mathcal{E}_{X}}(\lambda)-\mathbb{E}\left[\overline{\Delta\mathcal{E}_{X}}(\lambda)\right]\right|\geq t\right)\lesssim\mathrm{e}^{k^{\prime}\xi(t\min\{1/(\lambda+\lambda^{-1}),1\})}\;,

(310)

for a universal constant
k^{\prime}
. And, we conclude,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\Delta\mathcal{E}_{X}(\lambda)-\mathbb{E}\left[\overline{\Delta\mathcal{E}_{X}}(\lambda)\right]\right|>t\right)\lesssim\exp\left(-k^{\prime}\dfrac{c_{X}\eta^{3}n\min\{1,\lambda+\frac{1}{\lambda}\}^{2}t^{2}}{\max\{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}},1\}(\eta+c_{X}/d)\}}\right)+\exp\left(-c_{X}n\right)\;.

(311)

We now bound
\mathbb{E}\left[\overline{\Delta\mathcal{E}_{X}}(\lambda)\right]
, to this end, write,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\overline{\Delta\mathcal{E}_{X}}(\lambda)\right]

\displaystyle=-\dfrac{2(1-d/n)}{\lambda d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{X}(0)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right)+\mathbb{E}\left[\dfrac{2}{\lambda\mathfrak{b}(\lambda)d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]-\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right)

(312)

\displaystyle=-\dfrac{2(1-d/n)}{\lambda d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{X}(0)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right)+\mathbb{E}\left[\dfrac{2}{\lambda\mathfrak{b}(\lambda)d}\right]\operatorname{tr}\left(\left[\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right)-\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right)

(313)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{2}{\lambda}\operatorname{Cov}\left(\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{b}(\lambda)},\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right)

(314)

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\{-\dfrac{2(1-d/n)}{\lambda d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{b}^{*}(0)}(0)\right)+\dfrac{2}{\lambda\mathfrak{b}^{*}(\lambda)d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{b}^{*}(\lambda)}(\lambda)\right)-\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{b}^{*}(\lambda)}(\lambda)\right)\right\}\mathbb{P}\left(X\in\mathsf{A}_{\eta}\right)

(315)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{2}{\lambda}\operatorname{Cov}\left(\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{b}(\lambda)},\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right)

(316)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(1+\dfrac{d\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{n\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})}\right)\left(\dfrac{q\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})\eta^{6}}+\left(\dfrac{d\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{n}+\dfrac{d^{2}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{\eta n^{2}}\right)\mathrm{e}^{-c_{X}n}\right)

(317)

where the last line followed from applying the second deterministic equivalent presented in
Proposition

1
.

Recalling the definition of
\mathfrak{b}(\lambda)
, we have,

\displaystyle\dfrac{2}{\lambda}\operatorname{Cov}\left(\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{b}(\lambda)},\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{2\lambda d/n}{\lambda}\operatorname{Cov}\left(\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\right),\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\right)\right)

(318)

\displaystyle\leq 2\operatorname{Var}\left(\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\right)\right)\;,

(319)

which is controlled using
1
, from the fact that
\mathbf{X}\mapsto d^{-1}\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{\mathbf{X}}(\lambda)\right)
is
2\lambda^{-3/2}n^{-1/2}d^{1/2}
-lipschitz (from
lemma

6
), hence
1
ensures that
d^{-1}\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\right)
is sub Gaussian, and has variance bounded as,

\displaystyle\operatorname{Var}\left(\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(\lambda)\right)\right)\lesssim\dfrac{1}{\lambda^{3}nd}\;,

(320)

which implies,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\overline{\Delta\mathcal{E}_{X}}(\lambda)\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\{-\dfrac{2(1-d/n)}{\lambda d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{b}^{*}(0)}(0)\right)+\dfrac{2}{\lambda\mathfrak{b}^{*}(\lambda)d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{b}^{*}(\lambda)}(\lambda)\right)-\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{b}^{*}(\lambda)}(\lambda)\right)\right\}\mathbb{P}\left(X\in\mathsf{A}_{\eta}\right)

(321)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(1+\dfrac{d\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{n\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})}\right)\left(\dfrac{q\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})\eta^{6}}+\left(\dfrac{d\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{n}+\dfrac{d^{2}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{\eta n^{2}}\right)\mathrm{e}^{-c_{X}n}\right)+\dfrac{1}{\lambda^{3}nd}\;.

(322)

Now, we remark that by definition
\mathfrak{b}^{*}(0)
is the unique fixed point of
f_{0}(\mathfrak{b})=1+\mathfrak{b}d/n
, which gives,
\mathfrak{b}^{*}(0)=(1-d/n)^{-1}
, hence,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\overline{\Delta\mathcal{E}_{X}}(\lambda)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left\{-\dfrac{2}{\lambda d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\right)+\dfrac{2}{\lambda\mathfrak{b}^{*}(\lambda)d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{b}^{*}(\lambda)}(\lambda)\right)-\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{b}^{*}(\lambda)}(\lambda)\right)\right\}\mathbb{P}\left(X\in\mathsf{A}_{\eta}\right)

(323)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(1+\dfrac{d\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{n\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})}\right)\left(\dfrac{q\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})\eta^{6}}+\left(\dfrac{d\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{n}+\dfrac{d^{2}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{\eta n^{2}}\right)\mathrm{e}^{-c_{X}n}\right)+\dfrac{1}{\lambda^{3}nd}\;,

(324)

Finally, using the identity
\mathbf{A}^{-1}-\mathbf{B}^{-1}=\mathbf{A}^{-1}\{\mathbf{B}-\mathbf{A}\}\mathbf{B}^{-1}
, we get,

\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{b}^{*}(\lambda)}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{b}^{*}(\lambda)}(\lambda)-\Sigma_{X}^{-1}=\lambda\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{b}^{*}(\lambda)}(\lambda)\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\;,

(325)

we thus conclude on the bias of
\hat{\mathcal{E}}_{X}(\lambda)
as,

\mathbb{E}\left[\overline{\Delta\mathcal{E}_{X}}(\lambda)\right]\lesssim\underbrace{\left(1+\dfrac{d\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{n\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})}\right)\left(\dfrac{q\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})\eta^{6}}+\left(\dfrac{d\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{n}+\dfrac{d^{2}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{\eta n^{2}}\right)\mathrm{e}^{-c_{X}n}\right)+\dfrac{1}{\lambda^{3}nd}}_{B(n)}\;.

(326)

and, merging the previous equation with (
311
), we get a universal constant
k
, and the function
B(n)
defined above,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\Delta\mathcal{E}_{X}(\lambda)\right|>t+B(n)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\Delta\mathcal{E}_{X}(\lambda)\right|>t+\mathbb{E}\left[\overline{\Delta\mathcal{E}_{X}}(\lambda)\right]|\right)\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\Delta\mathcal{E}_{X}(\lambda)-\mathbb{E}\left[\overline{\Delta\mathcal{E}_{X}}(\lambda)\right]\right|\geq t\right)

(327)

\displaystyle\lesssim\exp\left(-k\dfrac{c_{X}\eta^{3}n\min\{1,\lambda+\frac{1}{\lambda}\}^{2}t^{2}}{\max\{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}},1\}(\eta+c_{X}/d)\}}\right)+\exp\left(-c_{X}n\right)\;.

(328)

which terminates the proof of
theorem

1
.

Appendix C
A deterministic equivalent for resolvent matrices of augmented sample covariances

In this section we generalize
Proposition

1
to the setting where a
non-negligible proportion of the dataset is produced by a data-augmentation scheme. We consider the
augmented dataset
[X_{1},\ldots,X_{n},G_{1},\ldots,G_{m}]
, where the two blocks

[X_{1},\ldots,X_{n}]
and
[G_{1},\ldots,G_{m}]
satisfy
2
–
6
.
The proof follows the non-augmented case presented in
Appendix

B
.

We begin with the following technical lemma, which will be used to derive concentration for the
augmented resolvent matrix
\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})
.

Lemma 7
.

Assume
X
and
\nu_{X}
satisfy
6
and
4
, let
f:\mathbb{R}^{d\times n}\times\mathbb{R}^{d\times m}\to\mathbb{R}
be a
\mathtt{L}_{f}
-Lispchitz function on
\mathsf{A}_{\eta}\times\mathbb{R}^{d\times m}
. Then for any
\mathbf{X},\mathbf{Y}\in\mathsf{A}_{\eta}
,

\left|\int f(\mathbf{X},g)\nu_{\mathbf{X}}^{\otimes m}(\mathrm{d}g)-\int f(\mathbf{Y},g)\nu_{\mathbf{Y}}^{\otimes m}(\mathrm{d}g)\right|\leq\mathtt{L}_{f}\left(1+\sqrt{m}\mathtt{L}_{G}\right)\|\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{Y}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\;.

(329)

i.e,
\mathbf{X}\mapsto\int f(\mathbf{X},g)d\nu_{\mathbf{X}}^{\otimes m}(dg)
is
\mathtt{L}_{f}(1+\mathtt{L}_{G})
Lispchitz on
\mathsf{A}_{\eta}
.

Proof.

Using
1
and
4
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\int f(\mathbf{X},g)\nu_{\mathbf{X}}^{\otimes m}(\mathrm{d}g)-\int f(\mathbf{Y},g)\nu_{\mathbf{Y}}^{\otimes m}(\mathrm{d}g)\right|

(330)

\displaystyle\leq\left|\int f(\mathbf{X},g)\nu_{\mathbf{X}}^{\otimes m}(\mathrm{d}g)-\int f(\mathbf{Y},g)\nu_{\mathbf{X}}^{\otimes m}(\mathrm{d}g)\right|+\left|\int f(\mathbf{Y},g)\nu_{\mathbf{X}}^{\otimes m}(\mathrm{d}g)-\int f(\mathbf{Y},g)\nu_{\mathbf{Y}}^{\otimes m}(\mathrm{d}g)\right|

(331)

\displaystyle\leq\mathtt{L}_{f}\left\|\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{Y}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\mathtt{L}_{f}W_{1}(\nu_{\mathbf{X}}^{\otimes m},\nu_{\mathbf{Y}}^{\otimes m})

(332)

\displaystyle\leq\mathtt{L}_{f}\left\|\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{Y}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\mathtt{L}_{f}\sqrt{m}\mathtt{L}_{G}\|\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{Y}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(333)

\displaystyle=\mathtt{L}_{f}\left(1+\sqrt{m}\mathtt{L}_{G}\right)\left\|\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{Y}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\;.

(334)

Where the last upper bound followed from
4
.
∎

We further recall the following notation for any positive semi-definite matrix
\mathbf{D}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
, and two dilation factors
\mathfrak{a}_{x}
and
\mathfrak{a}_{g}
.

\displaystyle\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D}):=\left(C_{\mathrm{Aug}}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}=\left((1-\alpha)C_{X}+\alpha C_{G}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}\;,

(335)

and,

\displaystyle\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x},\mathfrak{a}_{g})}(\mathbf{D}):=\left(\dfrac{1-(1-\beta/\mathfrak{a}_{g})\alpha}{\mathfrak{a}_{x}}\Sigma_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}}\bar{\Lambda}_{G}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}\;,

(336)

where
\bar{\Lambda}_{G}=\mathbb{E}\left[\Lambda_{G}(X)\right]
.

We prove the following result,

Theorem 4
.

Assume that
2
to
6
hold. Let
\mathbf{B}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
, and let
\mathbf{D}
be a positive semi-definite matrix. We define
(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*},\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})
as,

\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}=1+\dfrac{1-(1-\beta/\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})\alpha}{n}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right)\;,\quad\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}=1+\dfrac{\alpha}{m}\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\beta C_{X}+\Lambda_{G}(X)\right\}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right)\;,

(337)

Then, we have for a universal constant
k
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbf{B}\left\{\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)-\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\}\right)\right|\geq t\right)

(338)

\displaystyle\quad\leq 2\exp\left(-k\dfrac{n(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3}t^{2}}{\alpha(1+\sqrt{m}\mathtt{L}_{G})^{2}/d+(1-\alpha)\sigma_{G}^{2}/d+(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))\sigma_{G}^{2}}\right)\;.

(339)

And,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})-\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*},\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\mathbf{D})\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(340)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\dfrac{\alpha^{5}(\kappa q_{1}+q_{2})\sqrt{d}\left\{\sigma_{G}^{2}(\beta^{3}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}+\kappa^{3})+\sigma_{X}^{12}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})^{-1}n^{-1/2}d^{-1}\right\}}{n((1-\alpha)\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{6}}

(341)

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{\alpha\beta(1-\alpha^{\prime})^{-1}+1}{(1-\alpha)\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}\dfrac{\alpha\beta\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3/2}m}\left\{\dfrac{\sqrt{d}}{\sqrt{n+m}}(1+u(n))+\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{n+m}}+\sqrt{\alpha}\mathtt{L}_{G}+(1+c_{X}^{-1/2})\sqrt{\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}\right\}

(342)

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{\alpha\beta(1-\alpha^{\prime})^{-1}+1}{(1-\alpha)\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}\dfrac{\alpha\sqrt{d}\|\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3/2}m}\left(\dfrac{\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]\sqrt{\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}}{\|\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}+\sqrt{\alpha}\mathtt{L}_{G}+\dfrac{1+\sqrt{\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)

(343)

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]}{((1-\alpha)\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{2}}+\dfrac{1}{1-\alpha^{\prime}}\dfrac{q_{3}\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})/(1-\alpha^{\prime}))^{6}}

(344)

where
\alpha^{\prime}=\alpha-\alpha\beta/\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}
,
q_{1},q_{2}
and
q_{3}
are polynomials in
\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}),\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X}),\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{-1},c_{X}^{-1}
, and
n^{-1}
.

Proof.

Following the proof of
proposition

1
, we consider only the simpler case of
\sigma_{X}=1
(the general case readily follows by considering
X/\sigma_{X}
and
G/\sigma_{X}
.) we prove the two statements of the theorem independantly. First focusing on the concentration of
d^{-1}\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbf{B}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\right)
.

Proof of the concentration inequality.

Let
\mathbf{B}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
be any squared matrix, for notation simplicity we will denote
h_{\mathbf{B}}:\mathbf{X},\mathbf{G}\mapsto d^{-1}\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbf{B}\operatorname{R}_{\mathbf{X}\sqcup\mathbf{G}}(\mathbf{D})\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(\mathbf{X})
as well as
\tilde{X}=X\sqcup G
(for
\sqcup
being the column-wise concatenation operator), so that we simply need to bound the cumulative probality function of
h_{\mathbf{B}}(\tilde{X})-\mathbb{E}[h_{\mathbf{B}}(\tilde{X})]
.
To do so, we first bound its moment generating function, for any scalar
s\in\mathbb{R}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(s\left\{h_{\mathbf{B}}(\tilde{X})-\mathbb{E}\left[h_{\mathbf{B}}(\tilde{X})\right]\right\}\right)\right]

(345)

\displaystyle\quad=\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(s\left\{h_{\mathbf{B}}(\tilde{X})-\mathbb{E}\left[h_{\mathbf{B}}(\tilde{X})\mid X\right]\right\}\right)\cdot\exp\left(s\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[h_{\mathbf{B}}(\tilde{X})\mid X\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[h_{\mathbf{B}}(\tilde{X})\right]\right\}\right)\right]

(346)

\displaystyle\quad=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(s\left\{h_{\mathbf{B}}(\tilde{X})-\mathbb{E}\left[h_{\mathbf{B}}(\tilde{X})\mid X\right]\right\}\right)\,\middle|\,X\right]\cdot\exp\left(s\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[h_{\mathbf{B}}(\tilde{X})\mid X\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[h_{\mathbf{B}}(\tilde{X})\right]\right\}\right)\right]

(347)

Note that the random function
\mathbf{G}\mapsto h_{\mathbf{B}}(X\sqcup\mathbf{G})
is almost surely
2\|\mathbf{B}\|_{\mathrm{op}}(n+m)^{-1/2}d^{-1/2}(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{-3/2}
-Lispchitz on
\mathsf{A}_{\eta}
from
Lemma

6
, hence, relying on the
\sigma_{G}
-Lipschitz concentration property of
G
conditionally to
X
, and applying
Proposition

6
, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(s\left\{h_{\mathbf{B}}(\tilde{X})-\mathbb{E}\left[h_{\mathbf{B}}(\tilde{X})\mid X\right]\right\}\right)\,\middle|\,X\right]

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(-cs^{2}\sigma_{G}^{2}\left\{\dfrac{1}{(n+m)d(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3}}+\|h_{\mathbf{B}}\|_{\infty}^{2}\sigma_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}^{2}\right\}\right)\;.

(348)

Finally, remarking that under
2
, we have
\sigma_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}^{2}\lesssim n^{-1}
, as well as using
