\|h_{\mathbf{B}}\|_{\infty}\lesssim(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{-1}
, we have,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(s\left\{h_{\mathbf{B}}(\tilde{X})-\mathbb{E}\left[h_{\mathbf{B}}(\tilde{X})\mid X\right]\right\}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(-cs^{2}\sigma_{G}^{2}\left\{\dfrac{1}{(n+m)d(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3}}+\dfrac{1}{n(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{2}}\right\}\right)

(349)

\displaystyle=\exp\left(-cs^{2}\dfrac{\sigma_{G}^{2}}{n(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3}}\left\{\dfrac{1-\alpha}{d}+\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})\right\}\right)\;.

(350)

Now, we will leverage
Proposition

6
to bound the remaining term, writting
\mathbb{E}\left[h_{\mathbf{B}}(X)\mid X\right]=g_{\mathbf{B}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)
, where for any
\mathbf{X}\in{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}
,

g_{\mathbf{B}}(\mathbf{X})=\int\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbf{B}R_{\mathbf{X}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})\right)d\nu_{\mathbf{X}}^{\otimes m}(g)\;.

(351)

We know from
Lemma

7
that
g_{\mathbf{B}}
is
\mathtt{L}_{g_{\mathbf{B}}}
-Lispchitz on
\mathsf{A}_{\eta}
, with
\mathtt{L}_{g_{\mathbf{B}}}=2(1+\sqrt{m}\mathtt{L}_{G})(n+m)^{-1/2}d^{-1/2}(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{-3/2}
. Hence, using
Proposition

6
, we prove the existence of a numerical constant
c
, such the following bound holds,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(s\left\{g_{\mathbf{B}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)-\mathbb{E}\left[g_{\mathbf{B}}(X)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\}\right)\right]\leq\exp\left(-c_{2}s^{2}\dfrac{(1+\sqrt{m}\mathtt{L}_{G})^{2}}{d(n+m)(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3}}\right)\;,

(352)

Putting the previous bounds all together, we have shown that the moment generating function of
h_{\mathbf{B}}(X)
is bounded for a universal constant
c
by,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(s\left\{h_{\mathbf{B}}(\tilde{X})-\mathbb{E}\left[h_{\mathbf{B}}(\tilde{X})\right]\right\}\right)\right]

(353)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\exp\left(-cs^{2}\left\{\dfrac{(1+\sqrt{m}\mathtt{L}_{G})^{2}}{d(n+m)(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3}}+\dfrac{\sigma_{G}^{2}}{n(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3}}\left(\frac{1-\alpha}{d}+\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})\right)\right\}\right)

(354)

\displaystyle\quad=\exp\left(-cs^{2}\dfrac{1}{n(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3}}\left\{\dfrac{\alpha}{d}\left(1+\sqrt{m}\mathtt{L}_{G}\right)^{2}+\dfrac{(1-\alpha)\sigma_{X}^{2}}{d}+\sigma_{G}(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))\right\}\right)\;.

(355)

Relying on the Chernoff’s bound, and usual computations, the claimed concentration bound follows.

A first equivalent for
\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]
.

We now focus on the bias of
\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)
. We will show that
\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*},\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\mathbf{D})
is close to
\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]
.

As a first step, let us notice that conditionally to
X
,
G
satisfies all the assumptions of
Proposition

8
, hence for any
\sigma(X)
-measurable matrix
D_{X}
,
\operatorname{R}_{G}(D_{X})
admist a deterministic equivalent conditionally to
X
.
We will through a slight abuse of notation denote
\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}(D_{X})
the equivalent of
\operatorname{R}_{G}(D_{X})
conditionally to
X
. It is given by
Proposition

8
, i.e,

\displaystyle\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}(D_{X})

\displaystyle=\left(\dfrac{\mathbb{E}\left[C_{G}\mid X\right]}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+D_{X}\right)^{-1}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)=\left(\dfrac{\beta C_{X}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\dfrac{\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+D_{X}\right)^{-1}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\;,

(357)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)

\displaystyle=1+\dfrac{1}{m}\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[C_{G}\mid X\right]\mathbb{E}\left[R_{G}(D_{X})\mid X\right]\right)

(358)

\displaystyle=1+\dfrac{1}{m}\operatorname{tr}\left(\left\{\beta C_{X}+\Lambda_{G}(X)\right\}\mathbb{E}\left[R_{G}(D_{X})\mid X\right]\right)\;.

(359)

Writting for simplicity,
\alpha^{\prime}=(1-\beta/\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})\alpha
, and
\alpha^{\prime}(X)=(1-\beta/\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X))\alpha
, we will rely on the following upper bound which follows from the triangle inequality,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})-\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*},\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\mathbf{D})\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(360)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})-\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}\left(\dfrac{1-\alpha}{\alpha}C_{X}+\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\mathbf{D}\right)\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(361)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\bar{\operatorname{R}}^{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}_{G\mid X}\left(\dfrac{1-\alpha}{\alpha}C_{X}+\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\mathbf{D}\right)-\dfrac{1}{1-\alpha^{\prime}}\operatorname{R}_{X}\left(\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{(1-\alpha^{\prime})\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\dfrac{1}{1-\alpha^{\prime}}\mathbf{D}\right)\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(362)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\dfrac{1}{1-\alpha^{\prime}}\operatorname{R}_{X}\left(\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{(1-\alpha^{\prime})\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\dfrac{1}{1-\alpha^{\prime}}\mathbf{D}\right)-\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*},\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\mathbf{D})\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(363)

and, remark that we can rewrite,

\displaystyle\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})=\left((1-\alpha)C_{X}+\alpha C_{G}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}=\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\operatorname{R}_{G}\left(\dfrac{(1-\alpha)}{\alpha}C_{X}+\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\mathbf{D}\right)\;,

(364)

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}\left(\dfrac{1-\alpha}{\alpha}C_{X}+\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\mathbf{D}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\left(\dfrac{\mathbb{E}\left[C_{G}\mid X\right]}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\dfrac{1-\alpha}{\alpha}C_{X}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)

(365)

\displaystyle=\left(\left(1-\left(1-\dfrac{\beta}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}\right)\alpha\right)C_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)

(366)

\displaystyle=\left((1-\alpha^{\prime}(X))C_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\;,

(367)

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{1-\alpha^{\prime}}\operatorname{R}_{X}\left(\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{(1-\alpha^{\prime})\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\dfrac{1}{1-\alpha^{\prime}}\mathbf{D}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)=\left((1-\alpha^{\prime})C_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\;,

(368)

and,

\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*},\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)=\dfrac{1}{1-\alpha^{\prime}}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}}\left(\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{(1-\alpha^{\prime})\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\dfrac{1}{1-\alpha^{\prime}}\mathbf{D}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\;.

(369)

Using these equalities, we rewrite the previous bound as,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})-\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*},\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\mathbf{D})\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(370)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\operatorname{R}_{G}\left(\dfrac{(1-\alpha)C_{X}}{\alpha}+\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\mathbf{D}\right)-\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}\left(\dfrac{1-\alpha}{\alpha}C_{X}+\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\mathbf{D}\right)\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(371)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\left((1-\alpha^{\prime}(X))C_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}-\left((1-\alpha^{\prime})C_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(372)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\dfrac{1}{1-\alpha^{\prime}}\operatorname{R}_{X}\left(\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\dfrac{1}{1-\alpha^{\prime}}\mathbf{D}\right)-\dfrac{1}{1-\alpha^{\prime}}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}}\left(\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{(1-\alpha^{\prime})\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\dfrac{1}{1-\alpha^{\prime}}\mathbf{D}\right)\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(373)

The first and third terms in (
370
) are bounded by
Proposition

8
, whereas the second term is controlled from the fact that
\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)
and
\Lambda_{G}(X)
have small deviations. We deal with each of the terms in the right hand side of the previous upper bound one by one, first using the Jensen’s inequality,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\operatorname{R}_{G}\left(\dfrac{(1-\alpha)C_{X}}{\alpha}+\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\mathbf{D}\right)-\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}\left(\dfrac{1-\alpha}{\alpha}C_{X}+\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\mathbf{D}\right)\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(374)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\operatorname{R}_{G}\left(\dfrac{(1-\alpha)C_{X}}{\alpha}+\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\mathbf{D}\right)-\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G}^{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}\left(\dfrac{1-\alpha}{\alpha}C_{X}+\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\mathbf{D}\right)\right\}\middle|X\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]

(375)

Remarking that for all
\mathbf{X}\in\mathsf{A}_{\eta}
, we have
\uplambda_{d}((1-\alpha)/\alpha C_{\mathbf{X}}+\mathbf{D}/\alpha)\geq\alpha^{-1}((1-\alpha)\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))
. We set
\epsilon=(1-\alpha)\eta/\alpha+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})/\alpha
, we have from the previous remark and using
proposition

8
,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\operatorname{R}_{G}\left(\dfrac{(1-\alpha)C_{X}}{\alpha}+\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\mathbf{D}\right)-\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}\left(\dfrac{1-\alpha}{\alpha}C_{X}+\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\mathbf{D}\right)\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(376)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\operatorname{R}_{G}\left(\dfrac{(1-\alpha)C_{X}}{\alpha}+\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\mathbf{D}\right)-\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}\left(\dfrac{1-\alpha}{\alpha}C_{X}+\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\mathbf{D}\right)\right\}\middle|X\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]

(377)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\dfrac{\sigma_{G}^{2}}{\alpha}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{Q(\epsilon,\uplambda_{d}(\mathbb{E}\left[C_{G}\mid X\right]),\|\mathbb{E}\left[C_{G}\mid X\right]\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{-1},0,n^{-1})\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\|\mathbb{E}\left[C_{G}\mid X\right]\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{d}(\mathbb{E}\left[C_{G}\mid X\right])\epsilon^{6}}\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\;,

(378)

where
Q
is the polynomial function defined in
Proposition

8
. In order to integrate the above error over the distribution of
X
, one needs to ensure that this random quantity doesn’t blow up. We deal with this, first by using the fact that
Q
is non-decreasing with respect to it’s third entry, and we recall the notation
\kappa
, such that,

\mathop{Sp}(\Lambda_{G}(X))\subset[\kappa^{-1},\kappa]\quad\text{a.s}\;,

(379)

and using
\|\mathbb{E}\left[C_{G}\mid X\right]\|_{\mathrm{op}}\geq\operatorname{inf}_{\mathbf{X}}\|\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{X})\|_{\mathrm{op}}\geq\kappa
, we write,

\displaystyle Q(\epsilon,\uplambda_{d}(\mathbb{E}\left[C_{G}\mid X\right]),\|\mathbb{E}\left[C_{G}\mid X\right]\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{-1},0,n^{-1})

\displaystyle\leq Q(\epsilon,\uplambda_{d}(\mathbb{E}\left[C_{G}\mid X\right]),(\operatorname{inf}_{\mathbf{X}}\|\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{X})\|_{\mathrm{op}})^{-1},0,n^{-1})

(380)

\displaystyle\leq Q(\epsilon,\uplambda_{d}(\mathbb{E}\left[C_{G}\mid X\right]),\kappa,0,n^{-1})

(381)

and, remarking that there exists two polwnomials
q_{1}
and
q_{2}
(polynomials in
\epsilon,\kappa,n^{-1}
) such that,

Q(\epsilon,\uplambda_{d}(\mathbb{E}\left[C_{G}\mid X\right]),\kappa,0,n^{-1})=q_{1}+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbb{E}\left[C_{G}\mid X\right])q_{2}\;,

(382)

which is trivial from the definition of
Q
in
Proposition

1
. We write,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{Q(\epsilon,\uplambda_{d}(\mathbb{E}\left[C_{G}\mid X\right]),\|\mathbb{E}\left[C_{G}\mid X\right]\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{-1},0,n^{-1})\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\|\mathbb{E}\left[C_{G}\mid X\right]\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{d}(\mathbb{E}\left[C_{G}\mid X\right])\epsilon^{6}}\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]

(383)

\displaystyle\quad=q_{1}\mathbb{E}\left[q_{1}\dfrac{\|\mathbb{E}\left[C_{G}\mid X\right]\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{d}(\mathbb{E}\left[C_{G}\mid X\right])}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]+q_{2}\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\mathbb{E}\left[C_{G}\mid X\right]\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]}{n\epsilon^{6}}\;,

(384)

and, we control the remaining
\uplambda_{d}(\mathbb{E}\left[C_{G}\mid X\right])
term by remarking that
\uplambda_{d}(\mathbb{E}\left[C_{G}\mid X\right])=\uplambda_{d}(\beta C_{X}+\Lambda_{G}(X))\geq\operatorname{inf}_{\mathbf{X}}\uplambda_{d}(\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{X}))\geq\kappa^{-1}
, hence,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{Q(\epsilon,\uplambda_{d}(\mathbb{E}\left[C_{G}\mid X\right]),\|\mathbb{E}\left[C_{G}\mid X\right]\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{-1},0,n^{-1})\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\|\mathbb{E}\left[C_{G}\mid X\right]\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{d}(\mathbb{E}\left[C_{G}\mid X\right])\epsilon^{6}}\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]

(385)

\displaystyle\quad=\left(\kappa q_{1}+q_{2}\right)\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\mathbb{E}\left[C_{G}\mid X\right]\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]}{n\epsilon^{6}}\;,

(386)

It remains only to control the term
\mathbb{E}\left[\|\mathbb{E}\left[C_{G}\mid X\right]\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]
. Recall from
3
that
\mathbb{E}\left[C_{G}\mid X\right]=\beta C_{X}+\Lambda_{G}(X)
, which thanks to
(a+b)^{3}\lesssim a^{3}+b^{3}
implies,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\|\mathbb{E}\left[C_{G}\mid X\right]\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\beta^{3}\mathbb{E}\left[\|C_{X}-\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}\right]+\|\beta\Sigma_{X}+\Lambda_{G}(X)\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}

(387)

\displaystyle\lesssim\beta^{3}\mathbb{E}\left[\|C_{X}-\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}\right]+\beta^{3}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}+\kappa^{3}\;.

(388)

hence,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\operatorname{R}_{G}\left(\dfrac{(1-\alpha)C_{X}}{\alpha}+\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\mathbf{D}\right)-\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}\left(\dfrac{1-\alpha}{\alpha}C_{X}+\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\mathbf{D}\right)\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(389)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim(\kappa q_{1}+q_{2})\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\sigma_{G}^{2}(\beta^{3}\mathbb{E}\left[\|C_{X}-\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}\right]+\beta^{3}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}+\kappa^{3})}{n\alpha\epsilon^{6}}\;.

(390)

It remains only to handle the deviation of
C_{X}
in operator norm. To this end, we rely on
Vershynin 2018
result 9.2.5, which states that for a universal constant
K
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\|C_{X}-\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\geq K\sigma_{X}^{4}\left(\sqrt{\dfrac{r+u}{n}}+\dfrac{r+u}{n}\right)\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\right)\leq 2\mathrm{e}^{-u}\;,\quad r=\dfrac{\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\right)}{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}

(391)

Note that
Vershynin 2018
states this result in the case of
X
having sub-Gaussian columns, which in our setting is a direct consequence of
Definition

1
. In particular, we write,

\displaystyle\varphi(u)=K\sigma_{X}^{4}\left(\sqrt{\dfrac{r+u}{n}}+\dfrac{r+u}{n}\right)\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\;,\quad\text{thus}\quad\varphi^{\prime}(u)

\displaystyle=K\sigma_{X}^{4}\!\left(\frac{1}{2\sqrt{n(r+u)}}+\frac{1}{n}\right)\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\;.

(392)

and, by the change of variable
t=\varphi(u)
, we have,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|C_{X}-\Sigma_{X}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}\right]

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbb{P}\bigl(\|C_{X}-\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}\geq t\bigr)\,dt=\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbb{P}\bigl(\|C_{X}-\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\geq t^{1/3}\bigr)\,dt

(393)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbb{P}\bigl(\|C_{X}-\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\geq\varphi(u)\bigr)\;3\,\varphi(u)^{2}\,\varphi^{\prime}(u)\,du

(394)

\displaystyle\leq 2\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-u}\;3\,\varphi(u)^{2}\,\varphi^{\prime}(u)\,du\;\leq\;6\,\varphi^{\prime}(0)\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-u}\,\varphi(u)^{2}\,du

(395)

\displaystyle\leq 6\varphi^{\prime}(0)\dfrac{K^{2}\sigma_{X}^{8}}{n}\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathrm{e}^{-u}(r+u)+12\varphi^{\prime}(0)\dfrac{K^{2}\sigma_{X}^{8}}{n^{2}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathrm{e}^{-u}(r+u)^{2}

(396)

\displaystyle\leq 6K^{3}\sigma_{X}^{12}\left(\dfrac{1}{2\sqrt{n}r}+\dfrac{1}{n}\right)\left(\dfrac{r+1}{n}+\dfrac{2(r^{2}+2r+2)}{n^{2}}\right)\;,

(397)

recalling that
r=\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\right)/\|\Sigma_{x}\|_{\mathrm{op}}
, and noticing
d\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})/\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\leq r\leq d
, it results that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\|C_{X}-\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma_{X}^{12}\left(\dfrac{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{\sqrt{n}d\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})}+\dfrac{1}{n}\right)\left(\dfrac{d+1}{n}+\dfrac{d^{2}+d+2}{n^{2}}\right)

(398)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma_{X}^{12}\left(\dfrac{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{\sqrt{n}d\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})}+\dfrac{1}{n}\right)

(399)

where we have used
d<n
, garenteed by
2
. We plug this into (
389
), and we get,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\operatorname{R}_{G}\left(\dfrac{(1-\alpha)C_{X}}{\alpha}+\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\mathbf{D}\right)-\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}\left(\dfrac{1-\alpha}{\alpha}C_{X}+\dfrac{1}{\alpha}\mathbf{D}\right)\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(400)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim(\kappa q_{1}+q_{2})\left\{\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\sigma_{G}^{2}(\beta^{3}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}+\kappa^{3})}{n\alpha\epsilon^{6}}+\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\sigma_{G}^{12}}{n\alpha\epsilon^{6}}\left(\dfrac{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{\sqrt{n}d\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})}+\dfrac{1}{n}\right)\right\}

(401)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\dfrac{(\kappa q_{1}+q_{2})\sqrt{d}\left\{\sigma_{G}^{2}(\beta^{3}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}+\kappa^{3})+\sigma_{X}^{12}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})^{-1}n^{-1/2}d^{-1}\right\}}{n\alpha\epsilon^{6}}

(402)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\dfrac{\alpha^{5}(\kappa q_{1}+q_{2})\sqrt{d}\left\{\sigma_{G}^{2}(\beta^{3}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}+\kappa^{3})+\sigma_{X}^{12}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})^{-1}n^{-1/2}d^{-1}\right\}}{n((1-\alpha)\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{6}}

(403)

This concludes our analysis of the first term in (
370
). We now focus on the second term in
Equation

370
, which is controlled, provided
\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]
is small.

First, we check that,

\displaystyle\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)

\displaystyle=1+\dfrac{1}{m}\operatorname{tr}\left(\left\{\beta C_{X}+\Lambda_{G}(X)\right\}\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{G}\left((1-\alpha)C_{X}/\alpha+\mathbf{D}/\alpha\right)\mid X\right]\right)

(404)

\displaystyle=1+\dfrac{1}{m}\operatorname{tr}\left(\left\{\beta C_{X}+\Lambda_{G}(X)\right\}\mathbb{E}\left[\alpha\left((1-\alpha)C_{X}+\alpha C_{G}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}\mid X\right]\right)

(405)

\displaystyle=1+\dfrac{\alpha}{m}\operatorname{tr}\left(\left\{\beta C_{X}+\Lambda_{G}(X)\right\}\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\mid X\right]\right)

(406)

From this, one can hope that
\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)
concentrates around
\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)\right]
, hence, we write,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\left((1-\alpha^{\prime}(X))C_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}-\left((1-\alpha^{\prime})C_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(407)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\left((1-\alpha^{\prime}(X))C_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}-\left((1-\alpha^{\prime})C_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(408)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\left((1-\alpha^{\prime})C_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}-\left((1-\alpha^{\prime})C_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(409)

furthermore, relying on the identity
\mathbf{A}^{-1}-\mathbf{B}^{-1}
, we write,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\left((1-\alpha^{\prime}(X))C_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}-\left((1-\alpha^{\prime})C_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(410)

\displaystyle\quad=\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha^{\prime}(X)-\alpha^{\prime}\right)\left\{\left((1-\alpha^{\prime}(X))C_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}C_{X}\left((1-\alpha^{\prime})C_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(411)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\alpha^{\prime}(X)-\alpha^{\prime}\right|\left\|\left((1-\alpha^{\prime}(X))C_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}C_{X}\left((1-\alpha^{\prime})C_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\;,

(412)

Now, remarking that

\left\|C_{X}\left((1-\alpha^{\prime})C_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\leq\dfrac{1}{1-\alpha^{\prime}}\;,

(413)

and,

\left\|\left((1-\alpha^{\prime}(X))C_{X}+\alpha\dfrac{\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\leq\dfrac{1}{(1-\alpha^{\prime}(X))\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}\leq\dfrac{1}{(1-\alpha)\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}\;.

(414)

Similarly,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\left((1-\alpha^{\prime})C_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}-\left((1-\alpha^{\prime})C_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(415)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\alpha\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}-\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}\right|\left\|\left((1-\alpha^{\prime})C_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}\Lambda_{G}(X)\left((1-\alpha^{\prime})C_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]

(416)

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{\alpha}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\left((1-\alpha^{\prime})C_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}\left\{\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\right\}\left((1-\alpha^{\prime})C_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]

(417)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\dfrac{\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}\right|\right]}{(1-\alpha)\eta+\lambda_{1}(\mathbf{D})}+\dfrac{\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]}{((1-\alpha)\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{2}}\;,

(418)

which results in

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\left((1-\alpha^{\prime}(X))C_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}-\left((1-\alpha^{\prime})C_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(419)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\dfrac{\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\alpha^{\prime}(X)-\alpha^{\prime}\right|\right](1-\alpha^{\prime})^{-1}}{(1-\alpha)\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}+\dfrac{\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}\right|\right]}{(1-\alpha)\eta+\lambda_{1}(\mathbf{D})}+\dfrac{\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]}{((1-\alpha)\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{2}}

(420)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\dfrac{\alpha\beta\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}\right|\right](1-\alpha^{\prime})^{-1}}{(1-\alpha)\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}+\dfrac{\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}\right|\right]}{(1-\alpha)\eta+\lambda_{1}(\mathbf{D})}+\dfrac{\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]}{((1-\alpha)\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{2}}

(421)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\dfrac{\alpha\beta(1-\alpha^{\prime})^{-1}+1}{(1-\alpha)\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}\right|\right]+\dfrac{\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]}{((1-\alpha)\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{2}}\;.

(422)

From the previous, one can see that controlling
\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]
and
\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}\right|\right]
is sufficient in order to control the second term in decomposition (
370
). While the first needs to be assumed small, we can show that
\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{g}\right|\right]
is small under quite general conditions, to this end, we write,

\displaystyle\left|\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}\right|

(423)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\dfrac{\alpha\beta}{m}\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[C_{X}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\mid X\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[C_{X}\operatorname{R}_{Aug}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right)\right|

(424)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{\alpha}{m}\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\Lambda_{G}(X)\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\mid X\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\Lambda_{G}(X)\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right)\right|

(425)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\dfrac{\alpha\beta}{mn}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[X_{i}X_{i}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\mid X\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{i}X_{i}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right)\right|

(426)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{\alpha}{m}\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\Lambda_{G}(X)\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\mid X\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\Lambda_{G}(X)\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right)\right|\;.

(427)

Now remark that the distribution of
\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[X_{i}X_{i}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\mid X\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{i}X_{i}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\right]\right)\right|
doesn’t depend on
i
, by exchangeability of the columns of
X
and
5
, we thus focus only on the term
i=1
, by writting,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{\alpha\beta}{m}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\mid X\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right|\right]

(428)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{\alpha}{m}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\Lambda_{G}(X)\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\mid X\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\Lambda_{G}(X)\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right)\right|\right]

(429)

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{\alpha\beta}{m}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\mid X\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right|\right]

(430)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{\alpha}{m}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\{\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\}\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\mid X\right]\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)-\mathbb{E}\left[\{\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right)\right|\right]

(431)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{\alpha}{m}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\{\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\mid X\right]\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)-\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\}\right)\right|\right]

(432)

where the last inequality followed from using the triangle inequality. To bound the above quantity, we first focus on the second and third terms, which are notably less technical, it holds from Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and remarking that
\|\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\sqrt{d}(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{-1}
that

\displaystyle\dfrac{\alpha}{m}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\{\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\}\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\mid X\right]\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)-\mathbb{E}\left[\{\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right)\right|\right]

(433)

\displaystyle\quad\leq 2\dfrac{\alpha\sqrt{d}}{m(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]\;.

(434)

Furthermore, the function
\mathbf{X}\mapsto\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\int\operatorname{R}_{\mathbf{X}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})\right)d\nu_{\mathbf{X}}^{\otimes m}(g)
is
2\sqrt{d}\|\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{op}}(1+\sqrt{m}\mathtt{L}_{G})(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{-3/2}(n+m)^{-1/2}
-Lipschitz, from
lemma

6
and
lemma

7
. Hence, we have that
\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\mid X\right]\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}
is sub-Gaussian (which follows from
1
and
proposition

6
), and we have,

\displaystyle\dfrac{\alpha}{m}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\{\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\mid X\right]\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)-\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\}\right)\right|\right]

(435)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\dfrac{\alpha}{m}\sqrt{\operatorname{Var}(\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\mid X\right]\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X))}

(436)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\dfrac{\alpha}{m}\left(\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\|\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{op}}(1+\sqrt{m}\mathtt{L}_{G})}{\sqrt{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3}(n+m)}}+\dfrac{d\|\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}\sigma_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}\right)\;,

(437)

Recalling that
\sigma_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}\lesssim n^{-1}
from
2
, and using that
d<n
(which also follows from
2
), we simplify the previous bound

\displaystyle\dfrac{\alpha}{m}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\{\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\mid X\right]\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)-\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\}\right)\right|\right]

(438)

\displaystyle\lesssim\dfrac{\alpha\sqrt{d}\|\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3/2}m}\left(\dfrac{1+\sqrt{m}\mathtt{L}_{G}}{\sqrt{n+m}}+\sqrt{\dfrac{\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}{n}}\right)\lesssim\dfrac{\alpha\sqrt{d}\|\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3/2}m}\left(\sqrt{\alpha}\mathtt{L}_{G}+\dfrac{1+\sqrt{\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)

(439)

Plugging the previous calculation back into (
C
), we find,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\dfrac{\alpha\beta}{m}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\mid X\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right|\right]

(440)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{\alpha\sqrt{d}\|\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3/2}m}\left(\dfrac{\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]\sqrt{\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}}{\|\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}+\sqrt{\alpha}\mathtt{L}_{G}+\dfrac{1+\sqrt{\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)

(441)

It remains only to bound the expected deviation of
\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\mid X\right]
. Using the Shermann-morisson’s formula, we first write,

\displaystyle X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)

\displaystyle=\left\{X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup G}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}-\dfrac{1}{n+m}\dfrac{X_{1}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup G}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup G}(\lambda)X_{1}}{1+(n+m)^{-1}X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup G}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}}\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)

(442)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{X_{1}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup G}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}}{1+(n+m)^{-1}X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup G}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)

(443)

\displaystyle=\left\{(n+m)-\dfrac{(n+m)}{1+(n+m)^{-1}X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup G}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}}\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\;,

(444)

hence, writting
f:x\mapsto(n+m)/(1+(n+m)^{-1}x)
(note that
f
is
1
-Lipschitz), we have,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\mid X\right]

\displaystyle=(n+m)\mathbb{P}\left(X\in\mathsf{A}_{\eta}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[f(X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup G}(\mathbf{D})X_{1})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\mid X\right]

(445)

which allows to rewrite,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\mid X\right]

\displaystyle=\left\{(n+m)-\int f(X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})X_{1})d\nu_{X}^{\otimes m}(g)\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)

(447)

\displaystyle=(n+m)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)-\int f(X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})X_{1})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)

(448)

\displaystyle\quad+\int f(X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})X_{1})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)d\{\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}-\nu_{X}^{\otimes m}\}(g)

(449)

\displaystyle=(n+m)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)-f\left(\int X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right)

(450)

\displaystyle\quad-\left(\int f(X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})X_{1})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)-f\left(\int X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)

(451)

\displaystyle\quad+\int f(X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})X_{1})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)d\{\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}-\nu_{X}^{\otimes m}\}(g)\;,

(452)

which ensures,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)X_{1}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\mid X\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)X_{1}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right|\right]

(453)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\mathbb{E}\left[\left|f\left(\int X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})\,X_{1}\,d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)-\mathbb{E}\left[f\left(\int X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})\,X_{1}\,d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right|\right]

(454)

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\int f\bigl(X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})\,X_{1}\bigr)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\,d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)-f\!\Bigl(\!\int X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})\,X_{1}\,d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\Bigr)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right|\right]

(455)

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\int f\bigl(X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})\,X_{1}\bigr)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\,d\{\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}-\nu_{X}^{\otimes m}\}(g)\right|\right]\;,

(456)

and we once again bound each term in the previous upper bound (
453
), starting with the last term, we notice that the function
\mathbf{G}\mapsto f(X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup\mathbf{G}}(\mathbf{D})\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)X_{1})
is
2X_{1}^{\top}X_{1}(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{-3/2}(n+m)^{-1/2}
from
Lemma

6
(almsot surely). Hence, using
5
, we have,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\int f\bigl(X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})\,X_{1}\bigr)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\,d\{\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}-\nu_{X}^{\otimes m}\}(g)\right|\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\dfrac{\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\right]}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3/2}(n+m)^{1/2}}u(n)

(457)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\right)}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3/2}(n+m)^{1/2}}u(n)\;.

(458)

Furthermore, using the Jensen’s inequality, we have,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\int f\bigl(X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})\,X_{1}\bigr)\,d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)-f\!\Bigl(\!\int X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})\,X_{1}\,d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\Bigr)\right|\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]

(459)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\mathbb{E}\Biggl[\int\Bigl|f\bigl(X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})\,X_{1}\bigr)-f\!\Bigl(\!\int X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})\,X_{1}\,d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\Bigr)\Bigr|\,d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\Biggr]

(460)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\mathbb{E}\Biggl[\int\Bigl|\operatorname{tr}\Bigl(X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\;\Bigl\{\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})-\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})\,d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\Bigr\}\Bigr)\Bigr|\,d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\Biggr].

(461)

Relying on the
\sigma_{X}
-Lipschitz concentration property of
\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}
, we can bound the previous term using the fact that
\mathbf{G}\mapsto\operatorname{tr}\left(X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup\mathbf{G}}(\mathbf{D})\right)
is Lipschitz (from
Lemma

6
). We get,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\int f\bigl(X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})\,X_{1}\bigr)\,d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)-f\!\Bigl(\!\int X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})\,X_{1}\,d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\Bigr)\right|\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]

(462)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\dfrac{\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1}^{\top}X_{1}\right]}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3/2}(n+m)^{1/2}}=\dfrac{\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\right)}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3/2}(n+m)^{1/2}}

(463)

Now, focusing on the first term in (
453
), we write using the Jensen’s inequality as well as leveraging the Lipschitz property of
f
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|f\left(\int X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})\,X_{1}\,d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)-\mathbb{E}\left[f\left(\int X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})\,X_{1}\,d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right|\right]

(464)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left|f\left(\int X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})\,X_{1}\,d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)-f\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\int X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})\,X_{1}\,d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right|\right]

(465)

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left[\left|f\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\int X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})\,X_{1}\,d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)-\mathbb{E}\left[f\left(\int X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})\,X_{1}\,d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right|\right]

(466)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\int X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})\,X_{1}\,d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)-\mathbb{E}\left[\int X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})\,X_{1}\,d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right|\right]

(467)

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\int X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})\,X_{1}\,d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]-\int X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})\,X_{1}\,d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right|\right]

(468)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\operatorname{Var}\left(X_{1}^{\top}\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})\mathrm{d}\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)X_{1}\right)}

(469)

Now, we remark that
\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})\mathrm{d}\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)
is
\sigma(X^{-})
measureable, our
Proposition

7
applies, and we get,

\displaystyle\operatorname{Var}\left(X_{1}^{\top}\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})\mathrm{d}\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)X_{1}\right)\lesssim d\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}\left\{\dfrac{(1+\sqrt{m}\mathtt{L}_{G})^{2}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3}(n+m)}+\dfrac{1+c_{X}^{-1}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{2}}\right\}

(471)

Which implies,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|f\left(\int X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})\,X_{1}\,d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)-\mathbb{E}\left[f\left(\int X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\mathbf{D})\,X_{1}\,d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right|\right]

(472)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\left\{\dfrac{(1+\sqrt{m}\mathtt{L}_{G})}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3/2}\sqrt{n+m}}+\dfrac{1+c_{X}^{-1/2}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))}\right\}

(473)

Putting all these bounds together, and plugging them back in (
453
), we find that,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\mid X\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})X_{1}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right|\right]

(474)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\left\{\dfrac{(1+\sqrt{m}\mathtt{L}_{G})}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3/2}\sqrt{n+m}}+\dfrac{1+c_{X}^{-1/2}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))}\right\}

(475)

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\right)}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3/2}(n+m)^{1/2}}(1+u(n))

(476)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3/2}}\left\{\dfrac{\sqrt{d}}{\sqrt{n+m}}(1+u(n))+\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{n+m}}+\sqrt{\alpha}\mathtt{L}_{G}+(1+c_{X}^{-1/2})\sqrt{\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}\right\}

(477)

We conclude on the second term in (
370
) by plugging the above bound into (
440
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\dfrac{\alpha\beta\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3/2}m}\left\{\dfrac{\sqrt{d}}{\sqrt{n+m}}(1+u(n))+\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{n+m}}+\sqrt{\alpha}\mathtt{L}_{G}+(1+c_{X}^{-1/2})\sqrt{\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}\right\}

(478)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{\alpha\sqrt{d}\|\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3/2}m}\left(\dfrac{\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]\sqrt{\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}}{\|\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}+\sqrt{\alpha}\mathtt{L}_{G}+\dfrac{1+\sqrt{\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)

(479)

Hence, using (
419
), we have,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\left((1-\alpha^{\prime}(X))C_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}-\left((1-\alpha^{\prime})C_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\mathbf{D}\right)^{-1}\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(480)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\dfrac{\alpha\beta(1-\alpha^{\prime})^{-1}+1}{(1-\alpha)\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}\dfrac{\alpha\beta\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3/2}m}\left\{\dfrac{\sqrt{d}}{\sqrt{n+m}}(1+u(n))+\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{n+m}}+\sqrt{\alpha}\mathtt{L}_{G}+(1+c_{X}^{-1/2})\sqrt{\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}\right\}

(481)

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{\alpha\beta(1-\alpha^{\prime})^{-1}+1}{(1-\alpha)\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}\dfrac{\alpha\sqrt{d}\|\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3/2}m}\left(\dfrac{\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]\sqrt{\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}}{\|\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}+\sqrt{\alpha}\mathtt{L}_{G}+\dfrac{1+\sqrt{\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)

(482)

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]}{((1-\alpha)\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{2}}

(483)

Which conclude our analysis of the second term in (
370
).

Finally, we turn to the third and final term in (
370
), which is controlled using
Proposition

8
. First note that
\uplambda_{d}(\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}/\mathfrak{a}^{*}_{g}+\mathbf{D}/(1-\alpha^{\prime}))\geq\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})/(1-\alpha^{\prime})
, hence
Proposition

8
ensures that there exists a constant
q_{3}
that depends polynomially on
\cdots
, such that,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\dfrac{1}{1-\alpha^{\prime}}\operatorname{R}_{X}\left(\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\dfrac{1}{1-\alpha^{\prime}}\mathbf{D}\right)-\dfrac{1}{1-\alpha^{\prime}}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}}\left(\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{(1-\alpha^{\prime})\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\dfrac{1}{1-\alpha^{\prime}}\mathbf{D}\right)\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(484)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\dfrac{1}{1-\alpha^{\prime}}\dfrac{q_{3}\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})/(1-\alpha^{\prime}))^{6}}

(485)

Now putting our computations all together, in particular plugging (
400
), (
480
) and (
484
), in (
370
) we have shown,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})-\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*},\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\mathbf{D})\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(486)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\dfrac{\alpha^{5}(\kappa q_{1}+q_{2})\sqrt{d}\left\{\sigma_{G}^{2}(\beta^{3}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}+\kappa^{3})+\sigma_{X}^{12}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})^{-1}n^{-1/2}d^{-1}\right\}}{n((1-\alpha)\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{6}}

(487)

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{\alpha\beta(1-\alpha^{\prime})^{-1}+1}{(1-\alpha)\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}\dfrac{\alpha\beta\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3/2}m}\left\{\dfrac{\sqrt{d}}{\sqrt{n+m}}(1+u(n))+\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{n+m}}+\sqrt{\alpha}\mathtt{L}_{G}+(1+c_{X}^{-1/2})\sqrt{\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}\right\}

(488)

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{\alpha\beta(1-\alpha^{\prime})^{-1}+1}{(1-\alpha)\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}\dfrac{\alpha\sqrt{d}\|\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3/2}m}\left(\dfrac{\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]\sqrt{\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}}{\|\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}+\sqrt{\alpha}\mathtt{L}_{G}+\dfrac{1+\sqrt{\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)

(489)

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]}{((1-\alpha)\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{2}}+\dfrac{1}{1-\alpha^{\prime}}\dfrac{q_{3}\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})/(1-\alpha^{\prime}))^{6}}

(490)

To simplify the above upper bound, we use the fact that
1-\alpha^{\prime}\geq 1-\alpha
,
\alpha\leq 1
as well as
d<n
, which yields,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\mathbf{D})-\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*},\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\mathbf{D})\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(491)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\dfrac{\alpha^{5}(\kappa q_{1}+q_{2})\sqrt{d}\left\{\sigma_{G}^{2}(\beta^{3}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}+\kappa^{3})+\sigma_{X}^{12}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})^{-1}n^{-1/2}d^{-1}\right\}}{n((1-\alpha)\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{6}}

(492)

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{\alpha\beta(1-\alpha)^{-1}+1}{(1-\alpha)\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}\dfrac{\alpha\beta\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3/2}m}\left\{(1+u(n))+\sqrt{\alpha}\mathtt{L}_{G}+(1+c_{X}^{-1/2})\sqrt{\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}\right\}

(493)

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{\alpha\beta(1-\alpha)^{-1}+1}{(1-\alpha)\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}\dfrac{\alpha\sqrt{d}\|\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{(\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{3/2}m}\left(\dfrac{\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]\sqrt{\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}}{\|\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}+\sqrt{\alpha}\mathtt{L}_{G}+\dfrac{1+\sqrt{\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)

(494)

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]}{((1-\alpha)\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{2}}+\dfrac{(1-\alpha)^{5}q_{3}\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})((1-\alpha)\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{6}}

(495)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\sigma_{G}^{2}(\beta^{3}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}+\kappa^{3})}{n\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})((1-\alpha)\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{6}}(\kappa q_{1}+q_{2})\left\{\uplambda_{d}(\Sigma_{X})+\dfrac{\sigma_{X}^{10}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{d(\beta^{3}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}+\kappa^{3})\sqrt{n}}\right\}

(496)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(\dfrac{\alpha\beta}{1-\alpha}+1\right)\dfrac{\alpha\beta\sqrt{d}(\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}+q_{3}\|\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{op}})}{((1-\alpha)\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{5/2}m}\left\{(1+u(n))+\sqrt{\alpha}\,\mathtt{L}_{G}+(1+c_{X}^{-1/2})\,\sqrt{\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D})}\right\}

(497)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(\dfrac{\alpha\beta}{1-\alpha}+1\right)\left(\dfrac{1}{((1-\alpha)\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{2}}+\dfrac{\alpha\beta\sqrt{d}}{((1-\alpha)\eta+\uplambda_{d}(\mathbf{D}))^{5/2}m}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]

(498)

∎

Appendix D
Proof of
theorem

2

This section of this Appendix details the proof of
theorem

2
. First recall the definition of
\hat{\mathcal{E}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)
, for all
\lambda>0
:

\displaystyle\Phi_{1}(\mathbf{X})

\displaystyle=\dfrac{(1-d/n)}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{\mathbf{X}}(0)\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{X})}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(\mathbf{X})}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(\mathbf{X})\;,

(500)

\displaystyle\Phi_{2}(\mathbf{X})

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1-(1-\beta/\mathfrak{a}_{g}(\mathbf{X}))\alpha}{d\mathfrak{a}_{x}(\mathbf{X})}\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{g}(\mathbf{X}))}(\lambda,\mathbf{X})\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{X})}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(\mathbf{X})}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right)\;,

Where we have used the three notations,

\displaystyle\mathfrak{a}_{x}(\mathbf{X})

\displaystyle=1+\dfrac{1-(1-\beta/\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X))\alpha}{n}X_{1}^{\top}\int\operatorname{R}_{\mathbf{X}^{-}\sqcup g}d\nu_{\mathbf{X}}^{\otimes m}(g)X_{1}\;,

(501)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{a}_{g}(\mathbf{X})

\displaystyle=1+\dfrac{\alpha}{m}\operatorname{tr}\left(\{\beta C_{X}+\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{X})\}\int\operatorname{R}_{\mathbf{X}\sqcup g}(\lambda)\mathrm{d}\nu_{\mathbf{X}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right)\;,

and, for any
\mathfrak{a}\geq 1
,

\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{(\mathfrak{a})}(\lambda,\mathbf{X}):=\left(\left(1-\alpha\right)C_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)+\alpha\beta C_{X}}{\mathfrak{a}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\;.

(502)

Finally, we set,

\hat{\mathcal{E}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda):=\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)^{2}\right)-2(\Phi_{1}(X)-\Phi_{2}(X))+\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}^{-2}\right)\;,

(503)

Firstly, in
section

D.1
we detail the concentration of
\mathfrak{a}_{x}(X)
and
\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)
defined in (
501
). Secondly, in
section

D.2
, we show that
\Phi_{1}(X)
and
\Phi_{2}(X)
(defined in (
500
)) essencially have sub-Exponential tail, we provide an upper bound on their sub-Exponential norm. We then conclude on the proof of
theorem

2
in the last part of the Appendix.

D.1
Concentration of
\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)
and
\mathfrak{a}_{x}(X)

Proposition 9
.

Assume that
X
and
G
satisfy assumptions
1
to
2
.
Let
\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)
and
\mathfrak{a}_{x}(X)
defined as in (
501
), we set,

\displaystyle\zeta_{x}(t)

\displaystyle:=\min\left\{\dfrac{\lambda^{2}t^{2}}{(1-\alpha)},\lambda t,\dfrac{\lambda^{3}t^{2}}{\sigma_{G}^{2}(\sqrt{\alpha}\mathtt{L}_{G}+1/\sqrt{n+m})^{2}},\zeta_{x}\left(\dfrac{\lambda t}{\beta\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}\right)\right\}

(504)

\displaystyle\zeta_{g}(t)

\displaystyle=\min\left\{\dfrac{\lambda^{2}t^{2}}{\beta^{2}},\dfrac{\lambda t}{\beta},\dfrac{\lambda^{3}(n+m)t^{2}}{\beta^{2}(\sigma_{G}+u(n))^{2}},\dfrac{\sqrt{\lambda^{3}}t}{\beta(\sigma_{G}+u(n))},\dfrac{\lambda^{3}(n+m)^{2}t^{2}}{\alpha^{2}\left(\mathtt{L}_{\Lambda}/\sqrt{\lambda}+\sqrt{\alpha}\kappa\mathtt{L}_{G}+\kappa/\sqrt{n+m}\right)^{2}}+\dfrac{\ln(n)}{n+m}\right\}

as well as,

\displaystyle\delta_{g}

\displaystyle:=\dfrac{\alpha\beta}{m}\left(\dfrac{4(\sigma_{G}+u(n))\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\right)}{\sqrt{\lambda^{3}(n+m)}}+\dfrac{(1+\mathtt{L}_{G})}{\sqrt{\lambda^{3}(n+m)}}\right)\;,

(505)

\displaystyle\delta_{x}

\displaystyle:=\delta_{g}+\dfrac{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{\sqrt{n}}\left(\dfrac{2u(n)}{\lambda^{3/2}}+\dfrac{\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\right)}{\lambda(n+m)}\right)\;.

(506)

then the following holds for a universal constant
c>0
,

\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}\right|\geq t+\delta_{x}\right)\lesssim\exp\left(-c(n+m)\zeta_{x}(t)\right)\;,

(507)

and,

\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\mathfrak{a}_{x}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}\right|\geq t+\delta_{g}\right)\lesssim\exp\left(-c(n+m)\zeta_{g}(t)\right)\;.

(508)

Proof.

We first recall that
\lambda>0
and from (
501
),

\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)=1+\dfrac{\alpha\beta}{m}\operatorname{tr}\left(C_{X}\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\mid X\right]\right)+\dfrac{\alpha}{m}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Lambda_{G}(X)\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\mid X\right]\right)

(509)

and from
Theorem

4
,

\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}=1+\dfrac{\alpha\beta}{m}\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[C_{X}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\right]\right)+\dfrac{\alpha}{m}\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\Lambda_{G}(X)\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\right]\right)

(510)

we can thus write,

\displaystyle\left|\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}\right|

(511)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\dfrac{\alpha\beta}{m}\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[C_{X}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\mid X\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[C_{X}\operatorname{R}_{Aug}(\lambda)\right]\right)\right|+\dfrac{\alpha}{m}\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\Lambda_{G}(X)\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\mid X\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\Lambda_{G}(X)\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\right]\right)\right|

(512)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\dfrac{\alpha\beta}{mn}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[X_{i}X_{i}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\mid X\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{i}X_{i}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\right]\right)\right|+\dfrac{\alpha}{m}\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\Lambda_{G}(X)\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\mid X\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\Lambda_{G}(X)\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\right]\right)\right|\;.

(513)

Now remark that the distribution of
\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[X_{i}X_{i}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\mid X\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{i}X_{i}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\right]\right)\right|
doesn’t depend on
i
, by exchangeability of the columns of
X
, we thus focus only on the term
i=1
. Using the Shermann-morisson’s formula, we have,

\displaystyle X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)X_{1}

\displaystyle=X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup G}(\lambda)X_{1}-\dfrac{1}{n+m}\dfrac{X_{1}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup G}(\lambda)X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup G}(\lambda)X_{1}}{1+(n+m)^{-1}X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup G}X_{1}}

(514)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{X_{1}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup G}X_{1}}{1+(n+m)^{-1}X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup G}(\lambda)X_{1}}

(515)

\displaystyle=(n+m)-\dfrac{(n+m)}{1+(n+m)^{-1}X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup G}(\lambda)X_{1}}\;,

(516)

hence, writting
f:x\mapsto(n+m)/(1+(n+m)^{-1}x)
(note that
f
is
1
-Lipschitz), we have,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)X_{1}\mid X\right]

\displaystyle=(n+m)-\mathbb{E}\left[f(X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup G}(\lambda)X_{1})\mid X\right]

(517)

In order to derive the concentration of
\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)X_{1}\mid X\right]
, we mostly rely on the use of the Hanson-Wright inequality, which applies to quadratic forms of the shape
X_{1}^{\top}M(X^{-})X_{1}
with
M(X^{-})
being a
\sigma(X^{-})
measureable random matrix. To this end, we show that
\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)X_{1}\mid X\right]
is close to being of this form. We have,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)X_{1}\mid X\right]

\displaystyle=(n+m)-\int f(X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1})d\nu_{X}^{\otimes m}(g)

(519)

\displaystyle=(n+m)-\int f(X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1})d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)

(520)

\displaystyle\quad+\int f(X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1})d\{\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}-\nu_{X}^{\otimes m}\}(g)

(521)

\displaystyle=(n+m)-f\left(\int X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1}d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right)

(522)

\displaystyle\quad-\left(\int f(X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1})d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)-f\left(\int X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1}d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right)\right)

(523)

\displaystyle\quad+\int f(X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1})d\{\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}-\nu_{X}^{\otimes m}\}(g)\;,

(524)

Similarly, we write,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\tilde{X}}(\lambda)X_{1}\right]

\displaystyle=(n+m)-f\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\int X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1}d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]\right)

(525)

\displaystyle\quad-\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\int f(X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1})d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]-f\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\int X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1}d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]\right)\right)

(526)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\left[\int f(X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1})d\{\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}-\nu_{X}^{\otimes m}\}(g)\right]\;,

(527)

which ensures,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)X_{1}\mid X\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)X_{1}\right]\right|

(528)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left|f\left(\int X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1}d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right)-f\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\int X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1}d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]\right)\right|

(529)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left|\int f(X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1})d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)-f\left(\int X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1}d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right)\right|

(530)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\int f(X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1})d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]-f\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\int X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1}d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]\right)\right|

(531)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left|\int f(X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1})d\{\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}-\nu_{X}^{\otimes m}\}(g)\right|+\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\int f(X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1})d\{\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}-\nu_{X}^{\otimes m}\}(g)\right]\right|\;,

(532)

Now, using the Jensen’s inequality, as well as the
1
-Lispchtiz property of
f
, the previous equation implies,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)X_{1}\mid X\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)X_{1}\right]\right|

(533)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left|X_{1}^{\top}\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)X_{1}-\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right)\right|

(534)

\displaystyle\qquad+\int\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\left\{\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)-\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right\}\right)\right|d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)

(535)

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left[\int\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\left\{\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)-\mathbb{E}\left[\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]\right\}\right)\right|d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]

(536)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left|\int f(X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1})d\{\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}-\nu_{X}^{\otimes m}\}(g)\right|+\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\int f(X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1})d\{\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}-\nu_{X}^{\otimes m}\}(g)\right]\right|\;.

(537)

To bound the above, first remark that the map
g:\mathbf{X}\sqcup\mathbf{G}\mapsto\operatorname{tr}\left(X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathbf{X}^{-}\sqcup\mathbf{G}}\right)
is
2\|X_{1}\|_{2}^{2}\lambda^{-3/2}(n+m)^{-1/2}
conditionally on
X_{1}
(as a consequence of
lemma

6
), we have from
3
, that
X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1}
is sub-Gaussian conditionally to
X
, for
g\sim\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}
, and from the moment bounds for sub-Gaussian random variables,

\displaystyle\int\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\left\{\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)-\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right\}\right)\right|d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)

(538)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\sqrt{\int\left(\operatorname{tr}\left(X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\left\{\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)-\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right\}\right)\right)^{2}d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)}

(539)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\dfrac{2\sigma_{G}X_{1}^{\top}X_{1}}{\sqrt{\lambda^{3}(n+m)}}\;,

(540)

Similarly, and using a triangle inequality, we have,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\int\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\left\{\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)-\mathbb{E}\left[\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]\right\}\right)\right|d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]

(541)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\int\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\left\{\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)-\mathbb{E}\left[\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]\right\}\right)\right|d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]

(542)

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{2\sigma_{G}\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\right]}{\sqrt{\lambda^{3}(n+m)}}

(543)

and, using
Lemma

7
, we have that and the variance bound for sub-Gaussian random variables, we have,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\int\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\left\{\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)-\mathbb{E}\left[\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]\right\}\right)\right|d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]

(544)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\dfrac{2(1+\sqrt{m}\mathtt{L}_{G})+2\sigma_{G}\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1}^{\top}X_{1}\right]}{\sqrt{\lambda^{3}(n+m)}}=\dfrac{2(1+\sqrt{m}\mathtt{L}_{G})+2\sigma_{G}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\right)}{\sqrt{\lambda^{3}(n+m)}}

(545)

Furthermore, using
4
, and recalling that
\mathbf{G}\mapsto f(X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X\sqcup\mathbf{G}}(\lambda)X_{1})
is
2\|X_{1}\|_{2}^{2}\lambda^{-3/2}(n+m)^{-1/2}
-Lispchitz, the two final terms are bounded as,

\displaystyle\left|\int f(X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1})d\{\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}-\nu_{X}^{\otimes m}\}(g)\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2\|X_{1}\|_{2}^{2}\lambda^{-3/2}(n+m)^{-1/2}W_{1}(\nu_{X}^{\otimes m},\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m})

(546)

\displaystyle\leq 2\|X_{1}\|_{2}^{2}\lambda^{-3/2}(n+m)^{-1/2}\sqrt{m}u(n)\;,

(547)

and,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\int f(X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1})d\{\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}-\nu_{X}^{\otimes m}\}(g)\right|\right]\leq 2\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\right)\lambda^{-3/2}\alpha^{1/2}u(n)\;,

(548)

Merging all these together, we have shown,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)X_{1}\mid X\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)X_{1}\right]\right|

(549)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left|\int X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1}d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)-\mathbb{E}\left[\int X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1}d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]\right|

(550)

\displaystyle\qquad+(\sigma_{G}+\alpha u(n))\dfrac{2X_{1}^{\top}X_{1}}{\sqrt{\lambda^{3}(n+m)}}+(\sigma_{G}+u(n))\dfrac{2\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\right)}{\sqrt{\lambda^{3}(n+m)}}+\dfrac{2(1+\mathtt{L}_{G})}{\sqrt{\lambda^{3}(n+m)}}

(551)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left|X_{1}^{\top}\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)X_{1}-\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1}d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right)\right|

(552)

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{2(\sigma_{G}+u(n))}{\sqrt{\lambda^{3}(n+m)}}\left|X_{1}^{\top}X_{1}-\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\right)\right|+\dfrac{4(\sigma_{G}+u(n))\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\right)}{\sqrt{\lambda^{3}(n+m)}}+\dfrac{2(1+\mathtt{L}_{G})}{\sqrt{\lambda^{3}(n+m)}}

(553)

Finally, putting back the previous upper bound in (
511
), we have,

\displaystyle\left|\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{\alpha\beta}{nm}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[X_{i}X_{i}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\tilde{X}}(\lambda)\mid X\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{i}X_{i}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{\tilde{X}}(\lambda)\right]\right)\right|

(554)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{2\alpha\beta(\sigma_{G}+u(n))}{\sqrt{\lambda^{3}(n+m)}mn}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|X_{i}X_{i}^{\top}-\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\right)\right|+\dfrac{4\alpha\beta(\sigma_{G}+u(n))\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\right)+2\alpha\beta(1+\mathtt{L}_{G})}{\sqrt{\lambda^{3}(n+m)}m}

(555)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{\alpha}{m}\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\Lambda_{G}(X)\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\mid X\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\Lambda_{G}(X)\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\right]\right)\right|\;,

(556)

Applying a union bound, we have,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(|\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}|\geq t+\dfrac{\alpha\beta}{m}\left(\dfrac{4(\sigma_{G}+u(n))\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\right)}{\sqrt{\lambda^{3}(n+m)}}+\dfrac{(1+\mathtt{L}_{G})}{\sqrt{\lambda^{3}(n+m)}}\right)\right)

(557)

\displaystyle\quad\leq n\mathbb{P}\left(\left|X_{1}^{\top}\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)X_{1}-\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1}d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right)\right|\geq\dfrac{mt}{3\alpha\beta}\right)

(558)

\displaystyle\qquad+n\mathbb{P}\left(|X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}-\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\right)|\geq\dfrac{\sqrt{\lambda^{3}(n+m)}mt}{6\alpha\beta(\sigma_{G}+u(n))}\right)

(559)

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\Lambda_{G}(X)\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\mid X\right]\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{tr}\left(\Lambda_{G}(X)\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\right)\right]\right|\geq\dfrac{mt}{3\alpha}\right)\;.

(560)

We now bound each term that appears in the left side of the previous equation, beginning with the third term, remark that the function
g:\mathbf{X}\mapsto\operatorname{tr}\left(\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{X})\int\operatorname{R}_{\mathbf{X}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{\mathbf{X}^{\otimes m}}(g)\right)
is Lipschitz, and,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\Lambda_{G}(X)\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\mid X\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\Lambda_{G}(X)\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\right]\right)\right|\geq\dfrac{mt}{3\alpha}\right)=\mathbb{P}\left(\left|g(X)-\mathbb{E}\left[g(X)\right]\right|\geq\dfrac{mt}{3\alpha}\right)

(561)

indeed, writting for
\mathbf{X},\mathbf{Y}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times n}
,

\displaystyle\left|g(\mathbf{X})-g(\mathbf{Y})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\operatorname{tr}\!\left(\left\{\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{X})-\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{Y})\right\}\int\operatorname{R}_{\mathbf{X}\sqcup g}(\lambda)\,d\nu_{\mathbf{X}^{\otimes m}}(g)\right)\right|

(562)

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\operatorname{tr}\!\left(\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{Y})\left\{\int\operatorname{R}_{\mathbf{X}\sqcup g}(\lambda)\,d\nu_{\mathbf{X}^{\otimes m}}(g)-\int\operatorname{R}_{\mathbf{Y}\sqcup g}(\lambda)\,d\nu_{\mathbf{Y}^{\otimes m}}(g)\right\}\right)\right|

(563)

\displaystyle\leq\left(\dfrac{\mathtt{L}_{\Lambda}\sqrt{d}}{\lambda}+\dfrac{2\|\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{Y})\|_{\mathrm{op}}(1+\sqrt{m}\mathtt{L}_{G})\sqrt{d}}{\lambda^{3/2}\sqrt{(n+m)}}\right)\|\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{Y}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(564)

\displaystyle\leq\left(\dfrac{\mathtt{L}_{\Lambda}\sqrt{d}}{\lambda}+\dfrac{2\kappa(1+\sqrt{m}\mathtt{L}_{G})\sqrt{d}}{\lambda^{3/2}\sqrt{(n+m)}}\right)\|\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{Y}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\;.

(565)

where the last bounds were derived by using
4
,
Lemma

7
, and the fact that
\|\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{Y})\|_{\mathrm{op}}\leq\kappa
(as well as the fact that
\mathbf{X}\sqcup\mathbf{G}\mapsto\operatorname{R}_{\mathbf{X}\sqcup\mathbf{G}}(\lambda)
is
2\lambda^{-3/2}(n+m)^{-1/2}
-Lispchitz). Furthermore, note,

\displaystyle\dfrac{3\alpha}{m}\left(\dfrac{\mathtt{L}_{\Lambda}\sqrt{d}}{\lambda}+\dfrac{2\kappa(1+\sqrt{m}\mathtt{L}_{G})\sqrt{d}}{\lambda^{3/2}\sqrt{(n+m)}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{3}{\sqrt{\lambda^{3}(m+n)}}\left(\dfrac{\mathtt{L}_{\Lambda}}{\sqrt{\lambda}}+\dfrac{2\kappa(1+\sqrt{m}\mathtt{L}_{G})}{\sqrt{n+m}}\right)

(566)

\displaystyle\lesssim\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda^{3}(m+n)}}\left(\dfrac{\mathtt{L}_{\Lambda}}{\sqrt{\lambda}}+\sqrt{\alpha}\kappa\mathtt{L}_{G}+\dfrac{\kappa}{\sqrt{n+m}}\right)\;,

(567)

Hence, the third term in (
557
) is bounded by applying
1
, we get for a universal constant
k
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\operatorname{tr}\Bigl(\mathbb{E}\Bigl[\Lambda_{G}(X)\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\mid X\Bigr]-\mathbb{E}\Bigl[\Lambda_{G}(X)\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\Bigr]\Bigr)\right|\geq\dfrac{mt}{3\alpha}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\!\left(-k\dfrac{\lambda^{3}(n+m)^{3}t^{2}}{\left(\mathtt{L}_{\Lambda}/\sqrt{\lambda}+\sqrt{\alpha}\kappa\mathtt{L}_{G}+\kappa/\sqrt{n+m}\right)^{2}}\right)\;.

(568)

We now focus on the first term in (
557
), by using the Hanson-Wright inequality, we have for a universal constant
k
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|X_{1}^{\top}\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)X_{1}-\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1}d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right)\right|\geq\dfrac{mt}{3\alpha\beta}\right)

(569)

\displaystyle\quad\leq 2\exp\left(-k\min\left\{\dfrac{\lambda^{2}(n+m)^{2}t^{2}}{d\beta^{2}},\dfrac{\lambda(n+m)t}{\beta}\right\}\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-k(n+m)\min\left\{\dfrac{\lambda^{2}t^{2}}{\beta^{2}},\dfrac{\lambda t}{\beta}\right\}\right)\;,

(570)

where we have used the fact that
m/\alpha=(n+m)
, as well as
\|\operatorname{R}_{\mathbf{X}\sqcup\mathbf{G}}(\lambda)\|_{\mathrm{op}}\leq\lambda^{-1}
. Similarly for the second term in (
557
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}-\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\right)\right|\geq\dfrac{\sqrt{\lambda^{3}(n+m)}mt}{6\alpha\beta(\sigma_{G}+u(n))}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\left(-c\min\left\{\dfrac{\lambda^{3}(n+m)^{3}t^{2}}{\beta(\sigma_{G}+u(n))^{2}d},\dfrac{\sqrt{\lambda^{3}(n+m)^{3}}t}{\beta(\sigma_{G}+u(n))}\right\}\right)

(571)

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\left(-c(n+m)\min\left\{\dfrac{\lambda^{3}(n+m)t^{2}}{\beta^{2}(\sigma_{G}+u(n))^{2}},\dfrac{\sqrt{\lambda^{3}(n+m)}t}{\beta(\sigma_{G}+u(n))}\right\}\right)\;.

(572)

We conclude, by merging the three previous bounds in
Equation

557
, it holds for a universal constant
k>0
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}\right|\geq t+\dfrac{\alpha\beta}{m}\left(\dfrac{4(\sigma_{G}+u(n))\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\right)}{\sqrt{\lambda^{3}(n+m)}}+\dfrac{(1+\mathtt{L}_{G})}{\sqrt{\lambda^{3}(n+m)}}\right)\right)

(573)

\displaystyle\quad\leq 2n\exp\left(-k(n+m)\min\left\{\dfrac{\lambda^{2}t^{2}}{\beta^{2}},\dfrac{\lambda t}{\beta}\right\}\right)+2n\exp\left(-k(n+m)\min\left\{\dfrac{\lambda^{3}(n+m)t^{2}}{\beta^{2}(\sigma_{G}+u(n))^{2}},\dfrac{\sqrt{\lambda^{3}(n+m)}t}{\beta(\sigma_{G}+u(n))}\right\}\right)

(574)

\displaystyle\qquad+2\exp\!\left(-k(n+m)\dfrac{\lambda^{3}(n+m)^{2}t^{2}}{\alpha^{2}\left(\mathtt{L}_{\Lambda}/\sqrt{\lambda}+\sqrt{\alpha}\kappa\mathtt{L}_{G}+\kappa/\sqrt{n+m}\right)^{2}}\right)

(575)

Thus, only keeping the dominant term, define

\displaystyle\zeta_{g}(t)

\displaystyle=\min\left\{\dfrac{\lambda^{2}t^{2}}{\beta^{2}},\dfrac{\lambda t}{\beta},\dfrac{\lambda^{3}(n+m)t^{2}}{\beta^{2}(\sigma_{G}+u(n))^{2}},\dfrac{\sqrt{\lambda^{3}}t}{\beta(\sigma_{G}+u(n))},\dfrac{\lambda^{3}(n+m)^{2}t^{2}}{\alpha^{2}\left(\mathtt{L}_{\Lambda}/\sqrt{\lambda}+\sqrt{\alpha}\kappa\mathtt{L}_{G}+\kappa/\sqrt{n+m}\right)^{2}}+\dfrac{\ln(n)}{n+m}\right\}

(576)

\displaystyle\quad-\dfrac{\ln(n)}{n+m}\;,

(577)

we have shown, for a universal constant
c
,

\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}\right|\geq t+\dfrac{\alpha\beta}{m}\left(\dfrac{4(\sigma_{G}+u(n))\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\right)}{\sqrt{\lambda^{3}(n+m)}}+\dfrac{(1+\mathtt{L}_{G})}{\sqrt{\lambda^{3}(n+m)}}\right)\right)\leq 6\mathbb{P}\left(-c(n+m)\zeta_{g}(t)\right)\;,

(578)

We now turn to the concentration of
\mathfrak{a}_{x}(X)
, we have from (
26
) and the triangle inequality,

\displaystyle\left|\mathfrak{a}_{x}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\dfrac{1-(1-\beta/\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X))\alpha}{n}\operatorname{tr}\left(\{X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}-\Sigma_{X}\}\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{\mathbf{X}^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right)\right|

(579)

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\dfrac{1-(1-\beta/\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X))}{n}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\left\{\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)-\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\right]\right\}\right)\right|

(580)

\displaystyle\quad+\beta\alpha\left|\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}-\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}\right|\dfrac{1}{n}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\right]\right)

(581)
