\displaystyle\leq\left|\dfrac{1-\alpha}{n}\operatorname{tr}\left(\left\{X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}-\Sigma_{X}\right\}\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{\mathbf{X}^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right)\right|

(582)

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\dfrac{1-\alpha}{n}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\left\{\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)-\mathbb{E}\left[\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]\right\}\right)\right|

(583)

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\dfrac{1-\alpha}{n}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\right]\right\}\right)\right|

(584)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{\beta\alpha\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}d}{n\lambda}\left|\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}\right|

(585)

where we have used the fact that
\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)\geq\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}
, and
\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}\geq 1
, as well as the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality. Similarly as previously, we bound the deviation probability of each term independantly then use a union bound argument to conclude. First,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\dfrac{1-\alpha}{n}\operatorname{tr}\left(\left\{X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}-\Sigma_{X}\right\}\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right)\right|\geq t\right)

(586)

\displaystyle\quad=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\left\{X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}-\Sigma_{X}\right\}\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right)\right|\geq\dfrac{nt}{1-\alpha}\middle|X^{-}\right)\right]

(587)

\displaystyle\quad\leq 2\exp\left(-k\min\left\{\dfrac{\lambda^{2}n^{2}t^{2}}{(1-\alpha)^{2}d},\dfrac{\lambda nt}{(1-\alpha)}\right\}\right)

(588)

\displaystyle\quad\leq 2\exp\left(-k(n+m)\min\left\{\dfrac{\lambda^{2}t^{2}}{1-\alpha},\lambda t\right\}\right)\;,

(589)

which followed from the Hanson-Wright inequality. The second term is controlled by remarking that,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\dfrac{1-\alpha}{n}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\left\{\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)-\mathbb{E}\left[\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]\right\}\right)\right|\geq t\right)

(590)

\displaystyle\quad=\mathbb{P}\left(\left|g(X^{-})-\mathbb{E}\left[g(X^{-})\right]\right|\geq(n+m)t\right)

(591)

where
g:\mathbf{X}\mapsto\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\int\operatorname{R}_{\mathbf{X}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{\mathbf{X}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right)
is
2\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}(1+\sqrt{m}\mathtt{L}_{G})\lambda^{-3/2}(n+m)^{-1/2}
-Lispchitz, and so does
\mathbf{X}\mapsto g(\mathbf{X}^{-})
by composition of Lispchitz maps. It resutls from
1
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\dfrac{1-\alpha}{n}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\left\{\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)-\mathbb{E}\left[\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]\right\}\right)\right|\geq t\right)

(592)

\displaystyle\quad\leq 2\exp\left(-k\dfrac{(n+m)^{3}\lambda^{3}t^{2}}{d\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}(1+\sqrt{m}\mathtt{L}_{G})^{2}}\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-k\dfrac{(n+m)\lambda^{3}t^{2}}{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}(\sqrt{\alpha}\mathtt{L}_{G}+1/\sqrt{n+m})^{2}}\right)

(593)

The third term is bounded by using the Caucy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle\left|\dfrac{1-\alpha}{n}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\right]\right\}\right)\right|

(594)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\dfrac{(1-\alpha)\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\sqrt{d}}{n}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(595)

and, using the shermann-Morisson’s formula, it results,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(596)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\{\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}-\nu_{X}^{\otimes m}\}(g)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\int\left\{\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)-\operatorname{R}_{X\sqcup g}(\lambda)\right\}d\nu_{X}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(597)

\displaystyle\quad=\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\{\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}-\nu_{X}^{\otimes m}\}(g)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\dfrac{1}{n+m}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\int\dfrac{\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-1}\sqcup g}(\lambda)}{1+(n+m)^{-1}X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1}}d\nu_{X}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(598)

remarking that, for the Lowner order
\preceq
, we have,

\displaystyle\dfrac{\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-1}\sqcup g}(\lambda)}{1+(n+m)^{-1}X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1}}\preceq\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-1}\sqcup g}(\lambda)\;,

(599)

further using the facts that the Lowner order is preserved when integrating over the distribution of the random matrices, and that the Forbenius norm is increasing for the Lowner order on PSd matrices, we have,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\int\dfrac{\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-1}\sqcup g}(\lambda)}{1+(n+m)^{-1}X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1}}d\nu_{X}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-1}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]\right\|

(600)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\int\|\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)X_{1}\|_{2}^{2}d\nu_{X}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]

(601)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\dfrac{\|X_{1}\|_{2}^{2}}{\lambda}\right]

(602)

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\right)}{\lambda}\;,

(603)

Thus,

\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\{\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}-\nu_{X}^{\otimes m}\}(g)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\dfrac{\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\right)}{\lambda(n+m)}\;,

(604)

Finally, using the dual representation of the Frobeniusn norm,
5
, and the Lispchitz property of
g\mapsto\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)
we have,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\{\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}-\nu_{X}^{\otimes m}\}(g)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle=\sup_{\|\mathbf{B}\|_{\mathrm{F}}=1}\mathbb{E}\left[\int\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbf{B}\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)\right)d\{\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}-\nu_{X}^{\otimes m}\}(g)\right]

(605)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\|\mathbf{B}\|_{\mathrm{F}}=1}\dfrac{2\mathbb{E}\left[W_{1}(\nu_{X}^{\otimes m},\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m})\right]}{\lambda^{3/2}(n+m)^{1/2}}

(606)

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{2\sqrt{m}u(n)}{\lambda^{3/2}(n+m)^{1/2}}=\dfrac{2\sqrt{\alpha}u(n)}{\lambda^{3/2}}

(607)

we conclude on the third term by,

\displaystyle\left|\dfrac{1-\alpha}{n}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\right]\right\}\right)\right|

(608)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\dfrac{(1-\alpha)\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\sqrt{d}}{n}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(609)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\dfrac{(1-\alpha)\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\sqrt{d}}{n}\left(\dfrac{2u(n)}{\lambda^{3/2}}+\dfrac{\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\right)}{\lambda(n+m)}\right)

(610)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\dfrac{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{\sqrt{n}}\left(\dfrac{2\sqrt{\alpha}u(n)}{\lambda^{3/2}}+\dfrac{\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\right)}{\lambda(n+m)}\right)

(611)

finally, the deviation probability of
\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)
that appears in (
578
) was already controlled in the first part of the proof, we thus conclude throuh a union bound argument that,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\mathfrak{a}_{x}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}\right|\geq t+\dfrac{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{\sqrt{n}}\left(\dfrac{2\sqrt{\alpha}u(n)}{\lambda^{3/2}}+\dfrac{\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\right)}{\lambda(n+m)}\right)+\delta_{g}\right)

(612)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\dfrac{1-\alpha}{n}\operatorname{tr}\left(\left\{X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}-\Sigma_{X}\right\}\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right)\right|\geq\dfrac{t}{3}\right)

(613)

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\dfrac{1-\alpha}{n}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}\left\{\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)-\mathbb{E}\left[\int\operatorname{R}_{X^{-}\sqcup g}(\lambda)d\nu_{X^{-}}^{\otimes m}(g)\right]\right\}\right)\right|\geq\dfrac{t}{3}\right)

(614)

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}\left(\dfrac{\beta\alpha\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}d}{n\lambda}\left|\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}\right|\geq\dfrac{t}{3}+\delta_{g}\right)

(615)

\displaystyle\quad\leq 2\exp\left(-c(n+m)\min\left\{\dfrac{\lambda^{2}t^{2}}{(1-\alpha)},\lambda t\right\}\right)+2\exp\left(-k\dfrac{(n+m)\lambda^{3}t^{2}}{\sigma_{G}^{2}(\sqrt{\alpha}\mathtt{L}_{G}+1/\sqrt{n+m})^{2}}\right)

(616)

\displaystyle\qquad+6\exp\left(-c(n+m)\zeta_{g}\left(\dfrac{n\lambda t}{\beta\alpha\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}d}\right)\right)

(617)

\displaystyle\quad\leq 2\exp\left(-c(n+m)\min\left\{\dfrac{\lambda^{2}t^{2}}{(1-\alpha)},\lambda t\right\}\right)+2\exp\left(-k\dfrac{(n+m)\lambda^{3}t^{2}}{\sigma_{G}^{2}(\sqrt{\alpha}\mathtt{L}_{G}+1/\sqrt{n+m})^{2}}\right)

(618)

\displaystyle\qquad+6\exp\left(-c(n+m)\zeta_{g}\left(\dfrac{\lambda t}{\beta\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}\right)\right)

(619)

defining,

\zeta_{x}(t):=\min\left\{\dfrac{\lambda^{2}t^{2}}{(1-\alpha)},\lambda t,\dfrac{\lambda^{3}t^{2}}{\sigma_{G}^{2}(\sqrt{\alpha}\mathtt{L}_{G}+1/\sqrt{n+m})^{2}},\zeta_{x}\left(\dfrac{\lambda t}{\beta\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}\right)\right\}

(620)

the claim follows.
∎

D.2
Proof of
Theorem

2

This section is dedicated to the proof of
Theorem

2
. To this end, we define
\Delta\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)=\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)-\hat{\mathcal{E}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)
, and we notice that

\displaystyle\Delta\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)

\displaystyle=2\left\{-\dfrac{1}{d}tr\left(\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\right)+(\Phi_{1}(X)-\Phi_{2}(X))\right\}\;,

(621)

where
\Phi_{1}
and
\Phi_{2}
are defined in (
27
). The proof of
Theorem

2
goes in several step that we hereby describe. In the first step (
Section

D.2.1
), we provide tight concentration bounds for
\Phi_{1}
and
\Phi_{2}
. In a second step (
Section

D.2.2
), we shall bound
\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\right]
. Finally, in the third and last step (
Section

D.2.3
), we deduce the concentration property of
\Delta\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)

D.2.1
Concentration of
\Phi_{1}
and
\Phi_{2}

First recall the definitions of
\Phi_{1}(X)
and
\Phi_{2}(X)
from (
27
),

\displaystyle\Phi_{1}(\mathbf{X})

\displaystyle=\dfrac{(1-d/n)}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{\mathbf{X}}(0)\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{X})}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(\mathbf{X})}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(\mathbf{X})\;,

(622)

\displaystyle\Phi_{2}(\mathbf{X})

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1-(1-\beta/\mathfrak{a}_{g}(\mathbf{X}))\alpha}{d\mathfrak{a}_{x}(\mathbf{X})}\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{g}(\mathbf{X}))}(\lambda,\mathbf{X})\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{X})}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(\mathbf{X})}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right)\;,

We begin by introducing the auxilary functions
\Psi_{1}
,
\Psi_{2}
defined as follows,

\forall\mathbf{X}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times n}\;,\quad\begin{aligned} \Psi_{1}(\mathbf{X})&=\dfrac{1-(d/n)}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{\mathbf{X}}(0)\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{X})}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(\mathbf{X})\;,\\
\Psi_{2}(\mathbf{X})&=\dfrac{1-(1-\beta/\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})\alpha}{d\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}}\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda)\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{X})}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right)\;,\end{aligned}

(623)

where
(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*},\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})
were defined in
Theorem

4
.

This first part of the proof consists in showing that
\Phi_{1}(X)
and
\Phi_{2}(X)
are respectively close to
\Psi_{1}(X)
and
\Psi_{2}(X)
, and then showing that the functions
\Psi_{1}
and
\Psi_{2}
are Lipschitz, which will results in sub-Gaussian concentration bounds from
1
.

Concentration bounds for
\Phi_{1}(X)-\Psi_{1}(X)
and
\Phi_{2}(X)-\Psi_{2}(X)

We now show that
\Phi_{1}(X)-\Psi_{1}(X)
has sub-exponential tail, to do so, write the following almost sure decomposition,

\displaystyle|\Phi_{1}(X)-\Psi_{1}(X)|

(624)

\displaystyle\quad=\dfrac{1-(d/n)}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(0)\right)\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(0)\left\{\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}-\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right\}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)

(625)

\displaystyle\quad=\left|\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}-\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}\right|\dfrac{\alpha(1-(d/n))}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\operatorname{R}_{X}(0)\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\Lambda_{G}(X)\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)

(626)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left|\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}\right|\dfrac{\alpha(1-(d/n))}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)\eta\lambda}\left\|\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}

(627)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left|\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}\right|\dfrac{\alpha}{\eta\lambda}

(628)

This implies,

\mathbb{P}\left(|\Phi_{1}(X)-\Psi_{1}(X)|\geq t\right)\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}\right|\geq\dfrac{\eta\lambda t}{\alpha}\right)\;,

(629)

Leveraging
Proposition

9
, the previous implies in a straightwordard way that,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(|\Phi_{1}(X)-\Psi_{1}(X)|\geq t+\dfrac{\alpha\delta_{g}}{\eta\lambda}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}\right|\geq\dfrac{\eta\lambda t}{\alpha}+\delta_{g}\right)\lesssim\exp\left(-c(n+m)\zeta_{g}\left(\dfrac{\eta\lambda t}{\alpha}\right)\right)\;,

(630)

where
\zeta_{g}
was defined in (
9
).

Similarly, we write for
\Phi_{2}(X)-\Psi_{2}(X)
,

\displaystyle\left|\Phi_{2}(X)-\Psi_{2}(X)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}-\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}\right|\dfrac{\beta\alpha}{d\mathfrak{a}_{x}(X)}\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X))}(\lambda,X)\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right)

(631)

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}}-\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{x}(X)}\right|\dfrac{1-\alpha^{\prime}}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X))}(\lambda,X)\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right)

(632)

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\dfrac{1-\alpha^{\prime}}{d\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}}\operatorname{tr}\left(\left\{\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X))}(\lambda,X)-\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda,X)\right\}\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right)\right|

(633)

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\dfrac{1-\alpha^{\prime}}{d\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}}\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda,X)\left\{\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}-\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right\}\right)\right|

(634)

Recalling that
\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda,X)
was defined in (
25
), we further get, from
\mathbf{A}^{-1}-\mathbf{B}^{-1}=\mathbf{A}^{-1}(\mathbf{B}-\mathbf{A})\mathbf{B}^{-1}
, that,

\displaystyle\left|\Phi_{2}(X)-\Psi_{2}(X)\right|

(635)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left|\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}-\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}\right|\dfrac{\beta\alpha}{d\mathfrak{a}_{x}(X)}\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X))}(\lambda,X)\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right)

(636)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left|\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}}-\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{x}(X)}\right|\dfrac{1-\alpha^{\prime}}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X))}(\lambda,X)\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right)

(637)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left|\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}-\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}\right|\dfrac{1-\alpha^{\prime}}{d\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}}\operatorname{tr}\left(\left\{\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X))}(\lambda,X)\left(\alpha\beta C_{X}+\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)\right)\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda,X)\right\}\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right)

(638)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left|\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}-\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}\right|\dfrac{1-\alpha^{\prime}}{d\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}}\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda,X)\left\{\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right\}\right)

(639)

and, applying Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, as well as using that all dilation factors are greater than
1
, we get,

\displaystyle\left|\Phi_{2}(X)-\Psi_{2}(X)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}-\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)\right|\dfrac{\beta\alpha}{\lambda^{2}}

(640)

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}-\mathfrak{a}_{x}(X)\right|\dfrac{1-\alpha^{\prime}}{\lambda^{2}}

(641)

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}\right|\dfrac{1-\alpha^{\prime}}{\lambda^{2}}\left\|\dfrac{\alpha\beta C_{X}+\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda,X)\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}

(642)

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}\right|\dfrac{1-\alpha^{\prime}}{\lambda^{2}}\left\|\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}

(643)

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{\beta\alpha+2(1-\alpha^{\prime})}{\lambda^{2}}\left|\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}\right|+\dfrac{1-\alpha^{\prime}}{\lambda^{2}}|\mathfrak{a}_{x}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}|

(644)

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{1}{\lambda^{2}}|\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}|+\dfrac{1}{\lambda^{2}}|\mathfrak{a}_{x}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}|\;.

(645)

It results, from a unoin bound argument, that,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\Phi_{2}(X)-\Psi_{2}(X)\right|\geq t+\dfrac{\delta_{x}+\delta_{g}}{\lambda^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\mathfrak{a}_{g}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}\right|\geq\lambda^{2}t+\delta_{g}\right)+\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\mathfrak{a}_{x}(X)-\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}\right|\geq\lambda^{2}t+\delta_{x}\right)

(646)

\displaystyle\lesssim\exp\left(-c(n+m)\min\left\{\zeta_{x}(\lambda^{2}t),\zeta_{g}(\lambda^{2}t)\right\}\right)\;,

(647)

where the last line (as well as the definitions of
\delta_{x}
,
\delta_{g}
,
\zeta_{x}
and
\zeta_{g}
) followed from
Proposition

9
.

Concentration bounds for
\Psi_{1}(X)
and
\Psi_{2}(X)

As previously discussed, the concentration of
\Psi_{1}(X)
and
\Psi_{2}(X)
follows from the Lipschitz properties of
\Psi_{1}
and
\Psi_{2}
, as well as
1
. Starting by the concentration of
\Psi_{1}(X)
, we recall that
d<n
from
2
and we write for any
\mathbf{X},\mathbf{Y}\in\mathsf{A}_{\eta}
,

\displaystyle\left|\Psi_{1}(\mathbf{X})-\Psi_{1}(\mathbf{Y})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{1}{d}\,\mathrm{tr}\!\Bigl(\bigl\{R_{\mathbf{X}}(0)-R_{\mathbf{Y}}(0)\bigr\}\Bigl(\frac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{X})}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\Bigr)^{-1}\Bigr)\right|

(648)

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\frac{1}{d}\,\mathrm{tr}\!\Bigl(R_{\mathbf{Y}}(0)\Bigl\{\Bigl(\frac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{X})}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\Bigr)^{-1}-\Bigl(\frac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{Y})}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\Bigr)^{-1}\Bigr)\Bigr\}\right|

(649)

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{1}{d}\,\mathrm{tr}\!\Bigl(\bigl\{R_{\mathbf{X}}(0)-R_{\mathbf{Y}}(0)\bigr\}\Bigl(\frac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{X})}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\Bigr)^{-1}\Bigr)\right|

(650)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{\alpha}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}\left|\frac{1}{d}\,\mathrm{tr}\!\Bigl(R_{\mathbf{Y}}(0)\Bigl(\frac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{X})}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\Bigr)^{-1}\Bigl\{\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{X})-\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{Y})\Bigr\}\Bigl(\frac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{Y})}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\Bigr)^{-1}\Bigr)\right|

(651)

Using the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, as well as
4
, we get

\displaystyle\left|\Psi_{1}(\mathbf{X})-\Psi_{1}(\mathbf{Y})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{1}{\lambda}\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{d}}\|R_{\mathbf{X}}(0)-R_{\mathbf{Y}}(0)\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(652)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{\alpha}{\eta\lambda^{2}\sqrt{d}}\left\|\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{X})-\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{Y})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(653)

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\left(\dfrac{2}{\lambda\eta^{3/2}\sqrt{dn}}+\dfrac{\alpha\mathtt{L}_{\Lambda}}{\eta\lambda^{2}\sqrt{d}}\right)}_{\mathtt{L}_{\Psi_{1}}}\|\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{Y}\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

(654)

Where, we used
Lemma

6
and
4
in the last bound. We have proved that
\Psi_{1}
is
\mathtt{L}_{\Psi_{1}}
-Lispchitz on
\mathsf{A}_{\eta}
, and
\|\Psi_{1\mid\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}\|_{\infty}\leq\eta^{-1}\lambda^{-1}
, we have from
Proposition

6
that
\Psi_{1}(X)
is
\sigma_{1}
-sub-Gaussian, with

\displaystyle\sigma_{1}\lesssim\mathtt{L}_{\Psi_{1}}^{2}+\|\Psi_{1\mid\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}\|_{\infty}^{2}\sigma_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}^{2}\lesssim\dfrac{1}{\lambda\eta^{3/2}\sqrt{dn}}+\dfrac{\alpha\mathtt{L}_{\Lambda}}{\eta\lambda^{2}\sqrt{d}}\;,

(655)

Hence, there exists a constant
k>0
such that,

\mathbb{P}\left(|\Psi_{1}(X)-\mathbb{E}\left[\Psi_{1}(X)\right]|\geq t\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-k\dfrac{t^{2}}{(\lambda^{-1}\eta^{-3/2}/\sqrt{nd}+\mathtt{L}_{\Lambda}\eta^{-1}\lambda^{-2}/\sqrt{d})^{2}}\right)\;,

(656)

Similarly for
\Psi_{2}(X)
, we write for any
\mathbf{X},\mathbf{Y}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times n}
,

\displaystyle\left|\Psi_{2}(\mathbf{X})-\Psi_{2}(\mathbf{Y})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\left\{\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda,\mathbf{X})-\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda,\mathbf{Y})\right\}\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{X})}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right)

(657)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}d}\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda,\mathbf{Y})\left\{\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{X})}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}-\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{Y})}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right\}\right)\right|

(658)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\left\{\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda,\mathbf{X})-\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda,\mathbf{Y})\right\}\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{X})}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right)

(659)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{\alpha}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}d}\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda,\mathbf{Y})\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{X})}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\left\{\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{X})-\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{Y})\right\}\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{Y})}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right)\right|

(660)

and, using the Cauchy-Scharz inequality, we get,

\displaystyle\left|\Psi_{2}(\mathbf{X})-\Psi_{2}(\mathbf{Y})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}\lambda\sqrt{d}}\|\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda,\mathbf{X})-\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda,\mathbf{Y})\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(661)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{\alpha}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}\lambda^{2}\eta\sqrt{d}}\|\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{X})-\Lambda_{G}(\mathbf{Y})\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(662)

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\dfrac{2}{\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}\lambda\eta^{3/2}\sqrt{d(n+m)}}+\dfrac{\alpha\mathtt{L}_{\Lambda}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}\lambda^{2}\eta\sqrt{d}}}_{=\mathtt{L}_{\Psi_{2}}}\|\mathbf{X}-\mathbf{Y}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\;.

(663)

where we used the Lipschitz property of
\mathbf{X}\mapsto\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda,\mathbf{X})
, which follows from
Lemma

6
. Remark further that,

\displaystyle\mathtt{L}_{\Psi_{2}}\lesssim\mathtt{L}_{\Psi_{1}}\lesssim\dfrac{1}{\lambda\eta^{3/2}\sqrt{d(n+m)}}+\dfrac{\mathtt{L}_{\Lambda}}{\eta\lambda^{2}\sqrt{d}}\;,

(664)

thus, we get from
1
, the existence of a constant
k
such that,

\mathbb{P}\left(|\Psi_{2}(X)-\mathbb{E}\left[\Psi_{2}(X)\right]|\geq t\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-k\dfrac{t^{2}}{(\lambda^{-1}\eta^{-3/2}/\sqrt{d(n+m)}+\mathtt{L}_{\Lambda}\eta^{-1}\lambda^{-2}/\sqrt{d})^{2}}\right)\;,

(665)

D.2.2
An upper bound on the asymptotic bias of
\hat{\mathcal{E}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)

Let us denote for sake of notational simplicity,

\displaystyle\delta_{\mathrm{Aug}}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\sigma_{G}^{2}(\beta^{3}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}+\kappa^{3})}{n\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})((1-\alpha)\eta+\lambda)^{6}}(\kappa q_{1}+q_{2})\left\{\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})+\dfrac{1\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{d(\beta^{3}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}+\kappa^{3})\sqrt{n}}\right\}

(666)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\dfrac{\alpha\beta}{1-\alpha}+1\right)\dfrac{\alpha\beta\sqrt{d}(\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}+q_{3}\|\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{op}})}{((1-\alpha)\eta+\lambda)^{5/2}m}\left\{(1+u(n))+\sqrt{\alpha}\,\mathtt{L}_{G}+(1+c_{X}^{-1/2})\,\sqrt{\eta+\lambda}\right\}

(667)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\dfrac{\alpha\beta}{1-\alpha}+1\right)\left(\dfrac{1}{((1-\alpha)\eta+\lambda)^{2}}+\dfrac{\alpha\beta\sqrt{d}}{((1-\alpha)\eta+\lambda)^{5/2}m}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]

(668)

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\sigma_{G}^{2}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}(\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}+\kappa^{3})}{n\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})\lambda^{6}}(\kappa q_{1}+q_{2})\left\{\dfrac{\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})}{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}+\dfrac{1}{\kappa^{3}}\right\}

(669)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}+q_{3}\|\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{(1-\alpha)\lambda^{5/2}m}\left\{(1+u(n))+\sqrt{\alpha}\,\mathtt{L}_{G}+(1+c_{X}^{-1/2})\,\sqrt{\eta+\lambda}\right\}

(670)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\lambda^{1/2}+\dfrac{\sqrt{d}}{m}\right)\dfrac{\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|\mathrm{F}\right]}{(1-\alpha)\lambda^{5/2}}

(671)

such that it holds from
Theorem

4
,

\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)-\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*},\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda)\right\}\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\lesssim\delta_{\mathrm{Aug}}\;.

(672)

Let us first recall from (
621
),

\displaystyle\Delta\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)

\displaystyle=2\left\{-\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\right)+(\Phi_{1}(X)-\Phi_{2}(X))\right\}\;,

(673)

We have shown in
Section

D.2.1
that
\Phi_{1}(X)
and
\Phi_{2}(X)
respectively concentrate around
\mathbb{E}\left[\Psi_{1}(X)\right]
and
\mathbb{E}\left[\Psi_{2}(X)\right]
. In this section, we derive an upper bound for the aboslute value of

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{\Psi}(\lambda)\right]

\displaystyle=2\left\{-\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{R}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\right]\right)+\mathbb{E}\left[\Psi_{1}(X)\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\Psi_{2}(X)\right]\right\}\;,

(674)

First relying on (
672
), we write,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{\Psi}(\lambda)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|-\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*},\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda)\right)+\mathbb{E}\left[\Psi_{1}(X)\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\Psi_{2}(X)\right]\right|+\dfrac{\delta_{\mathrm{Aug}}}{\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})\sqrt{d}}\;,

(675)

and, we remark that in the case where
\Sigma_{X}
and
\bar{\Lambda}_{G}
commute, then the first term in the right-hand side can ’linearize’, in the general case, we use the notation
[\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B}]=\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}-\mathbf{B}\mathbf{A}
for the commutator of two matrices, and we write,

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*},\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda)\right)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\Bigl(\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*},\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda)\Bigl(\dfrac{\alpha\,\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\Bigr)\Bigl(\dfrac{\alpha\,\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\Bigr)^{-1}\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\Bigr)

(676)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*},\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda)\left(\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\left(\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right)

(677)

\displaystyle\quad-\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*},\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda)\left(\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\right)\left[\left(\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1},\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\right]\right).

(678)

We now bound the second term and provide a new expression for the first one.

Using the identity
[\mathbf{A}^{-1},\mathbf{B}^{-1}]=\mathbf{A}^{-1}\mathbf{B}^{-1}[\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B}]\mathbf{B}^{-1}\mathbf{A}^{-1}
, the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, and
\left\|\cdot\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\sqrt{d}\left\|\cdot\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}
, we can bound the term involving the commutator as

\displaystyle\left|\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*},\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda)\left(\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)\left[\left(\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1},\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\right]\right)\right|

(679)

\displaystyle\qquad=\left|\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*},\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda)\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\left[\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d},\Sigma_{X}\right]\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\left(\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right)\right|

(680)

\displaystyle\qquad=\alpha\left|\dfrac{1}{d\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*},\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda)\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\left[\bar{\Lambda}_{G},\Sigma_{X}\right]\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\left(\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right)\right|

(681)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\alpha\dfrac{\left\|\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\left(\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*},\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda)\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{\sqrt{d}\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}\|[\bar{\Lambda}_{G},\Sigma_{X}]\|_{\mathrm{F}}\lesssim\dfrac{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{\sqrt{d}\lambda^{2}}\left\|[\bar{\Lambda}_{G},\Sigma_{X}]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\;,

(682)

Furthermore, using
\mathbf{A}^{-1}-\mathbf{B}^{-1}=\mathbf{A}^{-1}(\mathbf{B}-\mathbf{A})\mathbf{B}^{-1}
we get,

\displaystyle\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*},\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda)\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)\Sigma_{X}^{-1}

\displaystyle=\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*},\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda)\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)\left((1-(1-\beta/\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})\alpha)\dfrac{\Sigma_{X}}{\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}}\right)^{-1}\dfrac{1-(1-\beta/\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})\alpha}{\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}}

(683)

\displaystyle=\Sigma_{X}^{-1}-\dfrac{(1-(1-\beta/\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})\alpha)}{\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*},\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda)\;.

(684)

Plugging (
682
)-(
684
) in (
678
), we get,

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*},\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda)\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\left(\dfrac{\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right)-\dfrac{1-(1-\beta/\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})\alpha}{\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}}\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*},\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda)\left(\dfrac{\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right)

(685)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{\sqrt{d}\lambda^{2}}\|[\bar{\Lambda}_{G},\Sigma_{X}]\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(686)

Plugging the previous equation in (
675
), we get,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{\Psi}(\lambda)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\left(\dfrac{\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[\Psi_{1}(X)\right]\right|

(687)

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\Psi_{2}(X)\right]-\dfrac{1-(1-\beta/\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})\alpha}{\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}}\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*},\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda)\left(\dfrac{\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right)\right|

(688)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{\sqrt{d}\lambda^{2}}\|[\bar{\Lambda}_{G},\Sigma_{X}]\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\dfrac{\delta_{\mathrm{Aug}}}{\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})\sqrt{d}}\;,

(689)

We thus simply need to bound the biases of
\Psi_{1}(X)
and
\Psi_{2}(X)
, for notation simplicity again, we introduce the notations,

\displaystyle\overline{\Psi}_{1}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\left(\dfrac{\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right)

(690)

\displaystyle\overline{\Psi}_{2}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1-(1-\beta/\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})\alpha}{\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}}\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*},\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda)\left(\dfrac{\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right)

(691)

Then, we have by definition of
\Psi_{1}(X)
in (
623
)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\Psi_{1}(X)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1-(d/n)}{d}\,\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{tr}\!\Bigl(R_{X}(0)\,\Bigl\{\Bigl(\frac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\Bigr)^{-1}\;-\;\Bigl(\frac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\Bigr)^{-1}\Bigr\}\Bigr)\,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]

(692)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1-(d/n)}{d}\,\mathrm{tr}\!\Bigl(\mathbb{E}\left[\Bigl\{R_{X}(0)\;-\;\frac{\Sigma_{X}^{-1}}{1-(d/n)}\Bigr\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\Bigl(\frac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\Bigr)^{-1}\Bigr)\,

(693)

\displaystyle\quad+\overline{\Psi}_{1}\mathbb{P}(X\in\mathsf{A}_{\eta})\;.

(694)

Thus, thanks to the triangle inequality,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\Psi_{1}(X)\right]-\bar{\Psi}_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{1}{d}\,\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{tr}\!\Bigl(R_{X}(0)\,\Bigl\{\Bigl(\frac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\Bigr)^{-1}\;-\;\Bigl(\frac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\Bigr)^{-1}\Bigr\}\Bigr)\,\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right|

(695)

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\frac{1}{d}\,\mathrm{tr}\!\Bigl(\mathbb{E}\left[\Bigl\{R_{X}(0)\;-\;\frac{\Sigma_{X}^{-1}}{1-(d/n)}\Bigr\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\Bigl(\frac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\Bigr)^{-1}\Bigr)\right|

(696)

\displaystyle\quad+\overline{\Psi}_{1}(1-\mathbb{P}(\mathsf{A}_{\eta}))\;.

(697)

furthermore, using the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and the Jensen’s inequality, we get

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\Psi_{1}(X)\right]-\bar{\Psi}_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{1}{\eta\sqrt{d}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\Bigl(\frac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\Bigr)^{-1}\;-\;\Bigl(\frac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\Bigr)^{-1}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]

(698)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{1}{\lambda\sqrt{d}}\,\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{R_{X}(0)\;-\;\frac{\Sigma_{X}^{-1}}{1-(d/n)}\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(699)

\displaystyle\quad+(1-\mathbb{P}(\mathsf{A}_{\eta}))\bar{\Psi}_{1}\;.

(700)

Using
\mathbf{A}^{-1}-\mathbf{B}^{-1}=\mathbf{A}^{-1}\{\mathbf{B}-\mathbf{A}\}\mathbf{B}^{-1}
, we can bound the first term in (
698
),

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\Bigl(\frac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\Bigr)^{-1}\;-\;\Bigl(\frac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\Bigr)^{-1}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\leq\dfrac{\alpha}{\lambda^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]\;,

(701)

the second term in (
698
) is controlled by applying
Proposition

8
, remark that
(1-(d/n))^{-1}
is the fixed point of
\mathfrak{b}\mapsto 1+\mathfrak{b}(d/n)
(which implies that
(1-(d/n))^{-1}\Sigma_{X}^{-1}=\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{X}^{\mathfrak{b}^{*}}(0))
, the second term is bounded as

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[R_{X}(0)\;-\;\frac{\Sigma_{X}^{-1}}{1-(d/n)}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathsf{A}_{\eta}}(X)\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(702)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\left(1+\dfrac{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})}\right)\left(\dfrac{q\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})\eta^{6}}+\left(\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}+\dfrac{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{\eta}\right)\mathrm{e}^{-c_{X}n}\right)\;,

(703)

for
q
being a polynomial function in
\eta+\uplambda_{1}(\mathbf{D}),\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X}),\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{-1},c_{X}^{-1}
, and
n^{-1}
.

Finally, the last term in (
698
) is controlled thanks to
2
, prcesicely, it holds,

\displaystyle(1-\mathbb{P}(\mathsf{A}_{\eta}))\bar{\Psi}_{1}\leq\dfrac{\|\Sigma_{X}^{-1}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{\lambda}e^{-c_{X}n}=\dfrac{1}{\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})\lambda}e^{-c_{X}n}\;.

(704)

Putting (
701
), (
702
) and (
704
) together in (
698
), we get,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\Psi_{1}(X)\right]-\bar{\Psi}_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\dfrac{1}{\lambda\sqrt{d}}\left(1+\dfrac{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})}\right)\left(\dfrac{q\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})\eta^{6}}+\left(\dfrac{1}{\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})\lambda}+\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}+\dfrac{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{\eta}\right)\mathrm{e}^{-c_{X}n}\right)

(705)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{\alpha}{\eta\lambda^{2}\sqrt{d}}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]

(706)

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(1+\dfrac{\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})}{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}\right)\dfrac{q\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{4}}{n\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})^{2}\lambda\eta^{6}}

(707)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(1+\dfrac{\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})}{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}\right)\left(\dfrac{1}{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}+\dfrac{\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})\min\{\lambda,\eta\}}{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}+\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})\right)\dfrac{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}\mathrm{e}^{c_{X}n}}{\lambda\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})^{2}\min\{\lambda,\eta\}\sqrt{d}}

(708)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{\alpha}{\eta\lambda^{2}\sqrt{d}}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]\;.

(709)

We now turn to the bias of
\Psi_{2}(X)
, recalling (
623
), and (
690
), we have,

\displaystyle\Psi_{2}(X)-\bar{\Psi}_{2}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1-\alpha^{\prime}}{\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda,X)\left\{\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}-\left(\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}^{*}_{g}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right\}\right)

(710)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{1-\alpha^{\prime}}{\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}d}\operatorname{tr}\left(\left\{\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda,X)-\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*},\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}\right\}\left(\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}^{*}_{g}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right)

(711)

thus, using the triangle inequality yields,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\Psi_{2}(X)\right]-\bar{\Psi}_{2}\right|

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1-\alpha^{\prime}}{\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}d}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda,X)\left\{\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}-\left(\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}^{*}_{g}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right\}\right)\right|\right]

(712)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{1-\alpha^{\prime}}{\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*}d}\left|\operatorname{tr}\left(\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda,X)\right]-\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*},\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda)\right\}\left(\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}^{*}_{g}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right)\right|\;,

(713)

and, further using the Cauchy-Scharz inequality, as well as
1-\alpha^{\prime}\leq 1
, we get,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\Psi_{2}(X)\right]-\bar{\Psi}_{2}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{1}{\lambda\sqrt{d}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\left(\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}-\left(\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{\mathfrak{a}^{*}_{g}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]

(714)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{1}{\lambda\sqrt{d}}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda,X)\right]-\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*},\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda)\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(715)

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{1}{\lambda^{3}\sqrt{d}}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]+\dfrac{1}{\lambda\sqrt{d}}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda,X)\right]-\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*},\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda)\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\;.

(716)

Now, using that
\bar{\operatorname{R}}_{G\mid X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*})}(\lambda,X)=(1-\alpha^{\prime})^{-1}R_{X}\left(\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)/((1-\alpha^{\prime})\mathfrak{b}_{g}^{*})+\lambda/(1-\alpha^{\prime})\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)
, we write,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\Psi_{2}(X)\right]-\bar{\Psi}_{2}\right|

(717)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\dfrac{1}{\lambda^{3}\sqrt{d}}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]

(718)

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{1}{\lambda\sqrt{d}}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\left((1-\alpha^{\prime})C_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}-\left((1-\alpha^{\prime})C_{X}+\dfrac{\alpha\Lambda_{G}(X)}{\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\lambda\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)^{-1}\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(719)

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{1}{(1-\alpha^{\prime})\lambda\sqrt{d}}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[R_{X}\left(\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{(1-\alpha^{\prime})\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\dfrac{\lambda}{1-\alpha^{\prime}}\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)\right]-\bar{R}_{X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*})}\left(\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{(1-\alpha^{\prime})\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\dfrac{\lambda}{1-\alpha^{\prime}}\operatorname{I}_{d}\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(720)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\dfrac{1}{\lambda^{3}\sqrt{d}}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]+\dfrac{1}{(1-\alpha^{\prime})\lambda\sqrt{d}}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[R_{X}\left(\mathbf{D}\right)\right]-\bar{R}_{X}^{(\mathfrak{a}_{x}^{*})}\left(\mathbf{D}\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(721)

Where, we have used the notation,

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}=\dfrac{\alpha\bar{\Lambda}_{G}}{(1-\alpha^{\prime})\mathfrak{a}_{g}^{*}}+\dfrac{\lambda}{1-\alpha^{\prime}}\operatorname{I}_{d}\;,

(722)

Finally, using
Proposition

8
, we get,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\Psi_{2}(X)\right]-\bar{\Psi}_{2}\right|

(723)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\dfrac{1}{(1-\alpha^{\prime})\lambda\sqrt{d}}\dfrac{q\sqrt{d}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})(\eta+\uplambda_{1}(\mathbf{D}))^{6}}+\dfrac{1}{\lambda^{3}\sqrt{d}}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]\;.

(724)

And, remarking that
\uplambda_{1}(\mathbf{D})\geq\lambda/(1-\alpha^{\prime})
, we finally get,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\Psi_{2}(X)\right]-\bar{\Psi}_{2}\right|\lesssim\dfrac{q\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})\lambda((1-\alpha^{\prime})\eta+\lambda)^{6}}+\dfrac{1}{\lambda^{3}\sqrt{d}}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]\;.

(725)

D.2.3
Conclusion on the proof of
Theorem

2

To conclude on the proof of
Theorem

2
, we plug (
687
), we first show that
\Phi_{1}(X)
concentrates around
\bar{\Psi}_{1}
(resp.
\Phi_{2}(X)
around
\bar{\Psi}_{2}
) with a bias of order
\delta_{\mathrm{Aug}}
(resp.
\delta_{\mathrm{Aug}}
). Introduce the following notations,

\displaystyle\delta_{\Psi_{1}}

\displaystyle:=\left(1+\dfrac{\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})}{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}\right)\dfrac{q\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{4}}{n\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})^{2}\lambda\eta^{6}}

(726)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(1+\dfrac{\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})}{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}\right)\left(\dfrac{1}{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}+\dfrac{\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})\min\{\lambda,\eta\}}{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}+\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})\right)\dfrac{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}\mathrm{e}^{c_{X}n}}{\lambda\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})^{2}\min\{\lambda,\eta\}\sqrt{d}}

(727)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{\alpha}{\eta\lambda^{2}\sqrt{d}}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]

(728)

\displaystyle\delta_{\Psi_{2}}

\displaystyle:=\dfrac{q\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}}{n\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})\lambda((1-\alpha^{\prime})\eta+\lambda)^{6}}+\dfrac{1}{\lambda^{3}\sqrt{d}}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]

(729)

and, we make the preliminary remark that, for some
C_{1}
,
C_{2}
and
C_{3}
independant of
n
,
d
,
m
, and that depend polynomially on
\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})
,
\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}
and
\min\{\lambda,\eta\}
, we have,

\displaystyle\delta_{\Psi_{1}}+\delta_{\Psi_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\dfrac{C_{1}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{4}}{n\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})^{2}\min\{\lambda,\eta\}^{7}}+\dfrac{C_{2}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}\mathrm{e}^{-c_{X}n}}{\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})^{2}\min\{\lambda,\eta\}^{2}\sqrt{d}}+\dfrac{\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]}{\min\{\eta,\lambda\}^{3}\sqrt{d}}

(730)

\displaystyle\lesssim\dfrac{C_{3}(1+c_{X}^{-1})\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{4}}{n\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})^{2}\min\{\lambda,\eta\}^{7}}+\dfrac{\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]}{\min\{\eta,\lambda\}^{3}\sqrt{d}}

(731)

From there, we bound
\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{\Psi}(\lambda)\right]
as,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{\Psi}(\lambda)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\bar{\Psi}_{1}-\mathbb{E}\left[\Phi_{1}(X)\right]\right|+\left|\bar{\Psi}_{1}-\mathbb{E}\left[\Phi_{1}(X)\right]\right|

(732)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{\sqrt{d}\lambda^{2}}\|[\bar{\Lambda}_{G},\Sigma_{X}]\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\dfrac{\delta_{\mathrm{Aug}}}{\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})\sqrt{d}}

(733)

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta_{\Psi_{1}}+\delta_{\Psi_{2}}+\dfrac{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{\sqrt{d}\lambda^{2}}\|[\bar{\Lambda}_{G},\Sigma_{X}]\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\dfrac{\delta_{\mathrm{Aug}}}{\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})\sqrt{d}}

(734)

\displaystyle\lesssim\dfrac{C_{3}(1+c_{X}^{-1})\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{4}}{n\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})^{2}\min\{\lambda,\eta\}^{7}}+\dfrac{\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]}{\min\{\eta,\lambda\}^{3}\sqrt{d}}++\dfrac{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{\sqrt{d}\lambda^{2}}\|[\bar{\Lambda}_{G},\Sigma_{X}]\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\dfrac{\delta_{\mathrm{Aug}}}{\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})\sqrt{d}}

(735)

Where the first inequality followed from (
687
), the second from (
705
) and (
725
), and the last one followed from (
730
).

Now, recalling (
671
), we have,

\displaystyle\dfrac{\delta_{\mathrm{Aug}}}{\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})\sqrt{d}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\dfrac{\sigma_{G}^{2}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\kappa(\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{3}+\kappa^{3})}{n\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})\lambda^{6}}(q_{1}+\dfrac{q_{2}}{\kappa})\left\{\dfrac{\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})}{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}+\dfrac{1}{\kappa^{3}}\right\}

(736)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}+q_{3}\|\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}{(1-\alpha)\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})\lambda^{5/2}m}\left\{(1+u(n))+\sqrt{\alpha}\,\mathtt{L}_{G}+(1+c_{X}^{-1/2})\,\sqrt{\eta+\lambda}\right\}

(737)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\lambda^{1/2}+\dfrac{\sqrt{d}}{m}\right)\dfrac{\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda}_{G}\|\mathrm{F}\right]}{(1-\alpha)\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})\lambda^{5/2}\sqrt{d}}

(738)

It results that for constants
C_{4},C_{5}
independant of
n
,
d
,
m
, and that depend polynomially on
\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})
,
\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}
,
\kappa
,
m/n
,
u(n)
,
\mathtt{L}_{G}
and
\min\{\lambda,\eta\}
, we have,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{\Psi}(\lambda)\right]\right|\leq C_{4}\dfrac{(1+\sigma_{G}^{2})(1+c_{X}^{-1})(\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{4}\kappa+\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\kappa^{4})}{(1-\alpha)n\uplambda_{1}(\Sigma_{X})^{2}\min\{\lambda,\eta\}^{7}}+C_{5}\dfrac{\mathbb{E}\left[\|\Lambda_{G}(X)-\bar{\Lambda_{G}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right]}{\min\{\eta,\lambda\}^{3}\sqrt{d}}+\dfrac{\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}}{\sqrt{d}\lambda^{2}}\|[\bar{\Lambda}_{G},\Sigma_{X}]\|_{\mathrm{F}}

(739)

Writting
\delta_{\mathrm{Total}}=\delta_{\Psi_{1}}+\delta_{\Psi_{2}}+\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}d^{-1/2}\lambda^{-2}\|[\bar{\Lambda}_{G},\Sigma_{X}]\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\delta_{\mathrm{Aug}}
, we write from a union bound,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\Delta_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)\right|\geq t+K\delta_{\mathrm{Total}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\Delta\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Aug}}(\lambda)-\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Aug}}^{\Psi}(\lambda)\right]\right|\geq t\right)

(740)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\Phi_{1}(X)-\mathbb{E}\left[\Psi(X)\right]\right|\geq\dfrac{t}{2}\right)+\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\Phi_{2}(X)-\mathbb{E}\left[\Psi_{2}(X)\right]\right|\geq\dfrac{t}{2}\right)

(741)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\Phi_{1}(X)-\Psi_{1}(X)\right|\geq\dfrac{t}{4}\right)+\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\Psi_{1}(X)-\mathbb{E}\left[\Psi_{1}(X)\right]\right|\geq\dfrac{t}{4}\right)

(742)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\Phi_{2}(X)-\Psi_{2}(X)\right|\geq\dfrac{t}{4}\right)+\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\Psi_{2}(X)-\mathbb{E}\left[\Psi_{2}(X)\right]\right|\geq\dfrac{t}{4}\right)

(743)

\displaystyle\lesssim\exp\left(-k(n+m)\min\left\{\zeta_{x}(\lambda^{2}t),\zeta_{g}(\lambda^{2}t),\zeta_{g}\left(\dfrac{\eta\lambda t}{\alpha}\right)\right\}\right)

(744)

\displaystyle\quad+\exp\left(-k\dfrac{t^{2}}{(\lambda^{-1}\eta^{-3/2}/\sqrt{d(n+m)}+\mathtt{L}_{\Lambda}\eta^{-1}\lambda^{-2}/\sqrt{d})^{2}}\right)

(745)

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(-k(n+m)\min\left\{\zeta_{x}(\lambda^{2}t),\zeta_{g}(\lambda^{2}t),\zeta_{g}\left(\dfrac{\eta\lambda t}{\alpha}\right)\right\}\right)

(746)

\displaystyle\quad+\exp\left(-k\dfrac{\eta^{3}\lambda^{2}dt^{2}}{(\mathtt{L}_{\Lambda}\sqrt{\eta}+1/\sqrt{(n+m)})^{2}}\right)

(747)

Where the last inequality followed from (
630
), (
646
), (
656
) and (
665
). The previous holds for large enough
K
and small enough
k
that are both universal constants.

To conclude the proof, it remains only to simplify the quantities
\delta_{\mathrm{Total}}
and
\zeta_{x}
,
\zeta_{g}
, in order to match the statement of
Theorem

2
. We start by simplifying the exponent in the concentration statement. First, defining
\varepsilon=\min\{\lambda,\eta,1\}
, we remark that,

\displaystyle\exp\left(-k(n+m)\min\left\{\zeta_{x}(\lambda^{2}t),\zeta_{g}(\lambda^{2}t),\zeta_{g}\left(\dfrac{\eta\lambda t}{\alpha}\right)\right\}\right)\leq\exp\left(-k(n+m)\min\left\{\zeta_{x}(\varepsilon^{2}t),\zeta_{g}(\varepsilon^{2}t)\right\}\right)

(748)

We recall the definition of
\zeta_{x}(t)
and
\zeta_{g}(t)
from
Proposition

9
,

\displaystyle\zeta_{x}(t)

\displaystyle:=\min\left\{\dfrac{\lambda^{2}t^{2}}{(1-\alpha)},\lambda t,\dfrac{\lambda^{3}t^{2}}{\sigma_{G}^{2}(\sqrt{\alpha}\mathtt{L}_{G}+1/\sqrt{n+m})^{2}},\zeta_{g}\left(\dfrac{\lambda t}{\beta\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}\right)\right\}

(749)

\displaystyle\zeta_{g}(t)

\displaystyle=\min\left\{\dfrac{\lambda^{2}t^{2}}{\beta^{2}},\dfrac{\lambda t}{\beta},\dfrac{\lambda^{3}(n+m)t^{2}}{\beta^{2}(\sigma_{G}+u(n))^{2}},\dfrac{\sqrt{\lambda^{4}}t}{\beta(\sigma_{G}+u(n))},\dfrac{\lambda^{3}(n+m)^{2}t^{2}}{\alpha^{2}\left(\mathtt{L}_{\Lambda}+\sqrt{\alpha}\sqrt{\lambda}\kappa\mathtt{L}_{G}+\kappa\sqrt{\lambda}/\sqrt{n+m}\right)^{2}}+\dfrac{\ln(n)}{n+m}\right\}

Note that in the worst case scenariom we have
(n+m)=1
, hence we write,

\displaystyle\zeta_{g}(t)

\displaystyle\geq\min\left\{\dfrac{\varepsilon^{2}t^{2}}{\beta^{2}},\dfrac{\varepsilon t}{\beta},\dfrac{\varepsilon^{3}(n+m)t^{2}}{\beta^{2}(\sigma_{G}+u(n))^{2}},\dfrac{\varepsilon^{2}t}{\beta(\sigma_{G}+u(n))},\dfrac{\varepsilon^{3}(n+m)^{2}t^{2}}{\alpha^{2}\left(\mathtt{L}_{\Lambda}+\sqrt{\alpha}\sqrt{\lambda}\kappa\mathtt{L}_{G}+\kappa\sqrt{\lambda}/\sqrt{n+m}\right)^{2}}+\dfrac{\ln(n)}{n+m}\right\}

(750)

\displaystyle\geq\varepsilon^{3}\min\left\{\dfrac{t^{2}}{\beta^{2}\varepsilon},\dfrac{t}{\beta\varepsilon^{2}},\dfrac{t^{2}}{\beta^{2}(\sigma_{G}+u(n))^{2}},\dfrac{t}{\beta\varepsilon(\sigma_{G}+u(n))},\dfrac{t^{2}}{\alpha^{2}\left(\mathtt{L}_{\Lambda}+\sqrt{\alpha}\sqrt{\lambda}\kappa\mathtt{L}_{G}+\kappa\sqrt{\lambda}\right)^{2}}\right\}

(751)

and

\displaystyle\zeta_{x}(t)\geq\varepsilon^{3}\min\left\{\dfrac{t^{2}}{\varepsilon(1-\alpha)},\dfrac{t}{\varepsilon^{2}},\dfrac{t^{2}}{\sigma_{G}^{2}(\sqrt{\alpha}\mathtt{L}_{G}+1)^{2}},\zeta_{g}\left(\dfrac{\varepsilon t}{\beta\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}}\right)\right\}

(752)

We From there, we define,

\displaystyle\xi_{1,x}=\varepsilon^{2}\hskip 28.45274pt\xi_{2,x}=\max\{\varepsilon(1-\alpha),\sigma_{G}^{2}(\sqrt{\alpha}\mathtt{L}_{G}+1)^{2}\}

(753)

and,

\displaystyle\xi_{1,g}=\max\{\beta^{2}\varepsilon,\beta\varepsilon(\sigma_{G}+u(n))\}\hskip 28.45274pt\xi_{2,g}=\max\{\beta^{2}\varepsilon,\beta^{2}(\sigma_{G}+u(n))^{2},\alpha^{2}\left(\mathtt{L}_{\Lambda}+\sqrt{\alpha}\sqrt{\lambda}\kappa\mathtt{L}_{G}+\kappa\sqrt{\lambda}\right)^{2}\}

(754)

This ensures that,

\displaystyle\zeta_{g}(\varepsilon^{2}t)\geq\varepsilon^{7}\min\left\{\dfrac{t}{\varepsilon^{2}\xi_{1,g}},\dfrac{t^{2}}{\xi_{2,g}}\right\}\geq\varepsilon^{9}\min\left\{\dfrac{t}{\varepsilon^{4}\xi_{1,g}},\dfrac{t^{2}}{\varepsilon^{2}\xi_{2,g}}\right\}

(755)

and,

\displaystyle\zeta_{x}(\varepsilon^{2}t)

\displaystyle\geq\varepsilon^{3}\min\left\{\dfrac{\varepsilon^{2}t}{\xi_{1,g}},\dfrac{\varepsilon^{4}t^{2}}{\xi_{2,g}},\dfrac{\varepsilon^{3}t}{\beta\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\xi_{1,g}},\dfrac{\varepsilon^{6}t^{2}}{\beta^{2}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}\xi_{2,g}}\right\}

(756)

\displaystyle\geq\varepsilon^{9}\min\left\{\dfrac{t}{\varepsilon^{4}\xi_{1,g}},\dfrac{t^{2}}{\varepsilon^{2}\xi_{2,g}},\dfrac{t}{\beta\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\varepsilon^{3}\xi_{1,g}},\dfrac{t^{2}}{\beta^{2}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}\xi_{2,g}}\right\}

(757)

Defining
\rho_{1}
and
\rho_{2}
such that,

\displaystyle\rho_{1}

\displaystyle=\max\left\{\varepsilon^{4}\xi_{1,g},\beta\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}\varepsilon^{3}\xi_{1,g},\varepsilon^{4}\xi_{1,g}\right\}\hskip 28.45274pt\rho_{2}

\displaystyle=\max\left\{\varepsilon^{2}\xi_{2,g},\beta^{2}\|\Sigma_{X}\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{2}\xi_{2,g},\varepsilon^{2}\xi_{2,g}\right\}

(758)

we have shown,

\displaystyle\exp\left(-k(n+m)\min\left\{\zeta_{x}(\lambda^{2}t),\zeta_{g}(\lambda^{2}t),\zeta_{g}\left(\dfrac{\eta\lambda t}{\alpha}\right)\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(-k(n+m)\min\left\{\zeta_{x}(\varepsilon^{2}t),\zeta_{g}(\varepsilon^{2}t)\right\}\right)

(759)

\displaystyle\lesssim n\mathrm{e}^{-k(n+m)t^{2}/\rho_{2}}+n\mathrm{e}^{-k(n+m)t/\rho_{1}}

(760)
